```python
from .kx import KX74, hub_pip as hub

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
PITCH, PLD, DEPTH, GROOVE_R = 5.0, 0.5715, 2.06, 1.60
BRG_6004 = dict(d=20.0, D=32.0, B=7.0)   # 61804 thin-section (C0 ≈ 2.3 kN, ESTIMATE); name kept for callers


def pulley(teeth: int, width: float = 16.0, flange: bool = True, bore: float = 0.0) -> Part:
    pd = teeth * PITCH / math.pi
    od = pd - 2 * PLD
    p = Cylinder(od / 2, width, align=MIN)
    rc = od / 2 - DEPTH + GROOVE_R
    for k in range(teeth):
        a = 2 * math.pi * k / teeth
        p -= Pos(rc * math.cos(a), rc * math.sin(a), 0) * Cylinder(GROOVE_R, width, align=MIN)
        p -= Pos((rc + GROOVE_R) * math.cos(a), (rc + GROOVE_R) * math.sin(a), 0) * Rot(0, 0, math.degrees(a)) * Box(1.6, 2 * GROOVE_R, width, align=MIN)
    if flange:
        for z in (-1.2, width):
            p += Pos(0, 0, z) * Cylinder(od / 2 + 2.0, 1.2, align=MIN)
    if bore:
        p -= Cylinder(bore / 2, 200)
    return p


def small_hub(teeth: int = 20, belt_w: float = 15.0) -> Part:
    """KX74 hub face on top (z ≥ 0 side is the cartridge), then a Ø20 journal, pulley, Ø20 journal (straddle)."""
    w = belt_w + 1.0
    b = BRG_6004
    top_j = Pos(0, 0, -b["B"] - 1) * Cylinder(b["d"] / 2, b["B"] + 1, align=MIN)          # journal under the hub
    pul = Pos(0, 0, -b["B"] - 1 - 1.2 - w) * pulley(teeth, w)
    bot_j = Pos(0, 0, -b["B"] - 1 - 1.2 - w - 1.2 - b["B"]) * Cylinder(b["d"] / 2, b["B"], align=MIN)
    h = Pos(0, 0, -0.0) * hub(KX74)
    hz = h.bounding_box().min.Z
    return Pos(0, 0, 0) * h + Pos(0, 0, hz) * (top_j + pul + bot_j) - Pos(0, 0, hz - 200) * Cylinder(4.2, 400, align=MIN)


def bearings_dummy(z_top: float, belt_w: float = 15.0) -> Part:
    b = BRG_6004
    w = belt_w + 1.0
    ring = Cylinder(b["D"] / 2, b["B"], align=MIN) - Cylinder(b["d"] / 2, b["B"], align=MIN)
    z1 = z_top - b["B"] - 1
    z2 = z1 - 1.2 - w - 1.2 - b["B"]
    return Pos(0, 0, z1) * ring + Pos(0, 0, z2) * ring


def belt_loop(pd_small: float, pd_big: float, centre: float, width: float = 15.0, t: float = 3.6) -> Part:
    """Belt as a solid band around two pitch circles (for renders): hull of two discs minus the inner hull."""
    def hull(r1, r2):
        a = math.asin((r2 - r1) / centre)
        L = math.hypot(centre, 0)
        body = Cylinder(r1, width, align=MIN) + Pos(centre, 0, 0) * Cylinder(r2, width, align=MIN)
        # quadrilateral between tangent points
        mid = (r1 + r2) / 2
        body += Pos(centre / 2, 0, 0) * Box(centre, 2 * mid * math.cos(a) * 0.999, width, align=MIN)
        return body
    return hull(pd_small / 2 + t / 2, pd_big / 2 + t / 2) - hull(pd_small / 2 - t / 2, pd_big / 2 - t / 2)


ECC = dict(od=38.0, e=1.0, t=7.0)   # printed eccentric bushing around each 61804: rotate to tension the belt, 1 lock screw


def eccentric_bushing() -> Part:
    """Bearing seat Ø32 offset by e inside an Ø38 ring with a lock tab: ±1 mm centre-distance adjustment (≈ ±2 mm belt
    length) without slots or a tensioning screw. ~5 min per stage instead of ~15 (ESTIMATE)."""
    e = ECC
    r = Cylinder(e["od"] / 2, e["t"], align=MIN) - Pos(e["e"], 0, 0) * Cylinder(BRG_6004["D"] / 2, e["t"], align=MIN)
    r += Pos(e["od"] / 2 + 3, 0, 0) * (Box(10, 8, 2.5, align=MIN) - Pos(2, 0, 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 3, align=MIN))
    return r


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# D13 small-pulley stage v2 (loop 37; external review #1 finding 1): assemblable from standard parts.
#   steel Ø12 shaft · 2 × 6801 (12×21×5) in eccentric bushings (wall ≥ 7 mm, was 2 mm) · off-the-shelf aluminium HTD 5M
#   pulley (set screws on a flat) · rigid flange coupling (clamp on the shaft, 4 × M4 into heat-set inserts in a pocket
#   under the printed KX74 hub: ≈ 5 MPa bearing at 12.7 N·m).
# Assembly: flange → hub (4 × M4); shaft into wall A's bearing, pulley slid on between the walls, shaft on into wall B's
# bearing; coupling clamped on the shaft end; belt on; tension with the bushings.
# Stage frame: KX face at z = 0, cartridge +Z, stack toward −Z. Part dimensions marked ESTIMATE until datasheets are in.
SHAFT_D = 12.0
BRG_6801 = dict(d=12.0, D=21.0, B=5.0)            # C0 ≈ 1.0 kN (ESTIMATE); 2 per stage straddle the pulley: ≈ 0.35 kN each at the 691 N peak
FLANGE = dict(od=32.0, t=6.0, bolt_r=12.0, clamp_od=22.0, clamp_l=12.0)   # rigid flange coupling, 12 mm bore (ESTIMATE)
AL_PULLEY = dict(flange_od=38.0, body_l=20.0, hub_d=24.0, hub_l=7.0)      # HTD 5M, 15 mm belt, 20T/18T (ESTIMATE)
ECC_V2 = dict(od=38.0, e=1.0, t=5.0)


def _v2_stack():
    from .kx import KX74, hub_pip
    hub_h = -hub_pip(KX74).bounding_box().min.Z                 # 12.2
    z = -hub_h - FLANGE["clamp_l"]                              # bottom of the coupling's clamp hub
    jA = (z - 0.5 - BRG_6801["B"], z - 0.5)
    p0 = jA[0] - 0.5                                            # top of the pulley
    pul = (p0 - AL_PULLEY["body_l"] - AL_PULLEY["hub_l"], p0)
    jB = (pul[0] - 0.5 - BRG_6801["B"], pul[0] - 0.5)
    return dict(hub_h=hub_h, jA=jA, jB=jB, pulley=pul, belt_c=p0 - AL_PULLEY["body_l"] / 2, end=jB[0] - 1.0)


V2 = _v2_stack()


def kx_hub_v2():
    """Printed KX74 hub with the flange-coupling pocket underneath (pocket depth = flange thickness) and 4 insert holes."""
    from .kx import KX74, hub_pip
    h = hub_pip(KX74)
    h -= Pos(0, 0, -V2["hub_h"]) * Cylinder(FLANGE["od"] / 2 + 0.3, FLANGE["t"], align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))
    for k in range(4):
        a = math.pi / 2 * k + math.pi / 4
        h -= Pos(FLANGE["bolt_r"] * math.cos(a), FLANGE["bolt_r"] * math.sin(a), -V2["hub_h"]) * Cylinder(2.8, FLANGE["t"] + 5, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))
    return h


def small_stage_v2(teeth: int = 20):
    """(name, part, colour) list in the stage frame. teeth: 20 (pitch, yaw, elbow, lift) or 18 (roll)."""
    MINZ = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
    v = V2
    flange = Pos(0, 0, -v["hub_h"]) * Cylinder(FLANGE["od"] / 2, FLANGE["t"], align=MINZ)
    flange += Pos(0, 0, -v["hub_h"] - FLANGE["clamp_l"]) * Cylinder(FLANGE["clamp_od"] / 2, FLANGE["clamp_l"], align=MINZ)
    flange -= Pos(0, 0, -200) * Cylinder(SHAFT_D / 2, 400, align=MINZ)
    shaft_top = -v["hub_h"] - FLANGE["clamp_l"] + 2
    shaft = Pos(0, 0, v["end"]) * Cylinder(SHAFT_D / 2, shaft_top - v["end"], align=MINZ)
    brg = lambda j: Pos(0, 0, j[0]) * (Cylinder(BRG_6801["D"] / 2, BRG_6801["B"], align=MINZ) - Cylinder(BRG_6801["d"] / 2, BRG_6801["B"], align=MINZ))
    pd = teeth * PITCH / math.pi
    p0, p1 = v["pulley"][1], v["pulley"][0]
    pul = Pos(0, 0, p0 - AL_PULLEY["body_l"]) * (Cylinder(pd / 2 - PLD, AL_PULLEY["body_l"], align=MINZ))
    for z in (p0 - 1.5, p0 - AL_PULLEY["body_l"]):
        pul += Pos(0, 0, z) * Cylinder(AL_PULLEY["flange_od"] / 2, 1.5, align=MINZ)
    pul += Pos(0, 0, p1) * Cylinder(AL_PULLEY["hub_d"] / 2, AL_PULLEY["hub_l"], align=MINZ)
    pul -= Pos(0, 0, -200) * Cylinder(SHAFT_D / 2, 400, align=MINZ)
    return [("kx_hub_v2", kx_hub_v2(), "dark"), ("flange_coupling", flange, "steel"), ("shaft_12", shaft, "steel"),
            ("brg_A_6801", brg(v["jA"]), "steel"), ("brg_B_6801", brg(v["jB"]), "steel"), ("al_pulley", pul, "steel")]


def eccentric_bushing_v2() -> Part:
    """Eccentric bushing for a 6801 (Ø21 seat, e = 1 mm) in the same Ø38 wall bore: thinnest wall ≈ 7.5 mm (was ≈ 2 mm)."""
    e = ECC_V2
    r = Cylinder(e["od"] / 2, e["t"], align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)) - Pos(e["e"], 0, 0) * Cylinder(BRG_6801["D"] / 2, e["t"], align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))
    r += Pos(e["od"] / 2 + 3, 0, 0) * (Box(10, 8, 2.5, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)) - Pos(2, 0, 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 3, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)))
    return r


# Loop 40 (R14): split big pulleys. A printed pulley slides onto its Ø40 tube between the walls and drives it through a
# double-D (two flats, 36 mm across): the tube still passes the 61808/6708 bores, so it can be inserted from one side.
DD_AF = 36.0


def dd_prism(h: float, af: float = DD_AF, r: float = 20.0) -> Part:
    """Double-D section (Ø2r with two flats af apart, flats normal to X) extruded along Z from z = 0."""
    return Cylinder(r, h, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)) & Box(af, 2 * r + 2, h, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))


def dd_flats(z0: float, h: float, af: float = DD_AF, r: float = 20.0) -> Part:
    """What to subtract from a Ø2r tube (axis Z) to cut the two flats over z0 … z0 + h."""
    return Pos(0, 0, z0) * (Cylinder(r + 1, h, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)) - dd_prism(h, af, r))


def split_pulley(teeth: int, width: float = 16.0, flange: bool = False, af: float = DD_AF, web: bool = True) -> Part:
    """Printed HTD 5M pulley with a double-D bore (+0.4 mm), lightened to a 5 mm web when it is large."""
    p = pulley(teeth, width, flange=flange) - dd_prism(width + 10, af + 0.4, 20.2).translate((0, 0, -5))
    r_rim = teeth * PITCH / math.pi / 2 - PLD - DEPTH - 5.0
    if web and r_rim > 34:
        for z in (0.0, width / 2 + 2.5):
            p -= Pos(0, 0, z) * (Cylinder(r_rim, width / 2 - 2.5, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)) - Cylinder(28, width / 2 - 2.5, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)))
    return p

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/cartridge_m.py ===
```python
"""Cartridge M: a GIM6010-8 (or a DM-J4310 with its own adapter) in a printed can.

Load path (KX v1): torque only. Output side: the KX74 male face on a motor adapter. Housing side:
a Ø6 steel torque pin that drops into a radial slot in the bone (precedent: shaft-mounted gearbox torque arm).
Only `motor_adapter` and the can's bolt circle depend on the motor; numbers marked ESTIMATE are
confirmed with `coupon_motor` (radial slots) when the first motor arrives.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

from build123d import Align, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, Axis, SortBy, fillet

from .kx import KX74, male, balls

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
MOTOR = dict(od=80.0, h=40.0,                     # with SDC104 driver (manual: Ø80 × 40)
             out_holes=8, out_pcd=34.0,           # 8×M3 on the output (count from the drawing; PCD ESTIMATE)
             rear_holes=6, rear_pcd=57.0)         # 6×M3 on the housing (count from the drawing; PCD ESTIMATE)
WALL, FLOOR = 3.0, 4.0
NECK, NECK_R = 5.0, 33.0      # spacer under the male flange: room for the collar teeth behind the lugs
TORQUE_PIN = dict(d=6.0, r=30.0, stick_out=10.0, ang=0.0)


def can() -> Part:
    od = MOTOR["od"] + 2 * WALL + 0.6
    h = MOTOR["h"] + FLOOR
    body = Cylinder(od / 2, h, align=MIN)
    body -= Pos(0, 0, FLOOR) * Cylinder(MOTOR["od"] / 2 + 0.3, h, align=MIN)
    body = fillet(body.edges().group_by(Axis.Z)[-1].sort_by(SortBy.RADIUS)[-1], 1.2)
    body = fillet(body.edges().group_by(Axis.Z)[0].sort_by(SortBy.RADIUS)[-1], 3.0)
    for k in range(0, MOTOR["rear_holes"], 2):            # loop 10 (R10): 3 of the 6 housing holes are enough (torque pin carries the torque)
        a = 2 * math.pi * k / MOTOR["rear_holes"]
        body -= Pos(MOTOR["rear_pcd"] / 2 * math.cos(a), MOTOR["rear_pcd"] / 2 * math.sin(a), 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 20)
        body -= Pos(MOTOR["rear_pcd"] / 2 * math.cos(a), MOTOR["rear_pcd"] / 2 * math.sin(a), 0) * Cylinder(3.1, 3.0)
    body -= Cylinder(13, 20)                                              # rear vent / cable pass-through
    body -= Pos(od / 2, 0, h / 2 + 4) * Box(12, 16, h - 8)                # side window: CAN + power pigtail
    body -= Pos(0, 0, h - 6) * (Cylinder(od / 2 + 1, 2) - Cylinder(od / 2 - 1.2, 2))   # light-ring groove
    tp = TORQUE_PIN
    body -= Pos(tp["r"], 0, 0) * Cylinder(tp["d"] / 2 - 0.05, 2 * FLOOR)  # press-fit seat for the steel torque pin
    return body


def torque_hanger(reach: float, width: float = 48.0, t: float = 7.0, flange: float = 26.0, slot_in: float = 6.0,
                  slot_out: float = 10.0) -> Part:
    """Standard torque-reaction part (confidence campaign): a printed plate with an open radial fork slot for the Ø6
    torque pin, hanging from a flange that screws to the nearest flat structure (2 × M4 into heat-set inserts).
    Local frame: pin axis +Z through the origin, radial direction +X (away from the cartridge axis), plate z ∈ [2, 2 + t],
    mount face at x = reach. The fork is open at the inner end (−X), so the cartridge keeps its radial float.
    Load: GIM peak 11 N·m / r 30 = 367 N along ±Y."""
    tp = TORQUE_PIN
    x0 = -slot_in - tp["d"] / 2
    p = Pos((x0 + reach) / 2, 0, 2) * Box(reach - x0, width, t, align=MIN)
    p += Pos(reach - 3, 0, 2 + t - flange) * Box(6, width, flange, align=MIN)                     # flange against the mount face
    for y in (-width / 2 + 8, width / 2 - 8):
        p -= Pos(reach - 3, y, 2 + t - flange / 2 - 3) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(2.2, 8)         # M4 clearance
    p -= Pos((x0 + slot_out) / 2 - 1, 0, 2) * Box(slot_out - x0 + 2, tp["d"] + 0.4, t, align=MIN)  # open fork slot
    return p


def torque_pin() -> Part:
    tp = TORQUE_PIN
    return Pos(tp["r"], 0, FLOOR - 0.5 - tp["stick_out"] - FLOOR + 0.5) * Cylinder(tp["d"] / 2, tp["stick_out"] + FLOOR - 0.5, align=MIN)


def motor_adapter() -> Part:
    """Bolts to the motor output (8×M3). Male face on top, mating face up (+Z) at z = NECK + face_t."""
    p = Cylinder(NECK_R, NECK, align=MIN) + Pos(0, 0, NECK + KX74.face_t) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * male(KX74, bore=8)
    for k in range(0, MOTOR["out_holes"], 2):             # loop 10 (R10): 4 of 8 output screws (11 N·m peak → 160 N shear per M3)
        a = 2 * math.pi * k / MOTOR["out_holes"] + math.pi / 8
        x, y = MOTOR["out_pcd"] / 2 * math.cos(a), MOTOR["out_pcd"] / 2 * math.sin(a)
        p -= Pos(x, y, 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 40)
        p -= Pos(x, y, NECK + KX74.face_t - 3.5) * Cylinder(3.1, 10, align=MIN)   # counterbores
    return p


def adapter_balls() -> Part:
    return Pos(0, 0, NECK + KX74.face_t) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * balls(KX74)


def motor_dummy() -> Part:
    return Pos(0, 0, FLOOR) * Cylinder(MOTOR["od"] / 2, MOTOR["h"], align=MIN)


def stack_height() -> float:
    """Can floor to the KX mating plane."""
    return FLOOR + MOTOR["h"] + NECK + KX74.face_t


def coupon_motor() -> Part:
    """3 mm slotted disc: bolt it to the motor's front and back faces to read the real bolt circles."""
    p = Cylinder(MOTOR["od"] / 2, 3, align=MIN) - Cylinder(5, 3, align=MIN)
    for k in range(6):                                   # housing pattern: slots spanning PCD 48–66
        p -= Rot(0, 0, 60 * k) * Pos(28.5, 0, 0) * Box(9 + 3.4, 3.4, 6)
    for k in range(8):                                   # output pattern: slots spanning PCD 26–42
        p -= Rot(0, 0, 45 * k + 22.5) * Pos(17, 0, 0) * Box(8 + 3.4, 3.4, 6)
    for k, r in enumerate(range(13, 42, 2)):             # engraved ruler: one tick per 2 mm of radius
        p -= Rot(0, 0, 30) * Pos(r, 0, 2.6) * Box(0.6, 3 if k % 5 else 5, 1)
    return p

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/cartridge_s.py ===
```python
"""Cartridge S: an STS3215 in a printed sleeve. The KX44 male face bolts to the servo's Ø20 disc horn (4×M3 on a 9.9 mm square).
Torque pin under the sleeve, as on cartridge M. Servo geometry measured from the SO-ARM100 model (cad/params.scad)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from build123d import Align, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, Axis, fillet

from .kx import KX44, male, balls

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
SERVO = dict(l=45.4, w=24.8, case_h=32.0, bottom=4.0, shaft_off=10.2, horn_d=20.0, horn_t=1.6, horn_sq=9.9)
FIT, WALL, FLOOR = 0.3, 2.4, 2.5
NECK, NECK_R = 4.0, 18.0
TORQUE_PIN = dict(d=4.0, x=22.0, stick_out=8.0)


def _servo_x0():   # x of the servo's near end, with the shaft on the z axis
    return -SERVO["shaft_off"]


def sleeve() -> Part:
    s = SERVO
    L, W = s["l"] + 2 * (FIT + WALL), s["w"] + 2 * (FIT + WALL)
    H = s["case_h"] + s["bottom"] + FLOOR
    cx = _servo_x0() + s["l"] / 2
    body = Pos(cx, 0, 0) * Box(L, W, H, align=MIN)
    body = fillet(body.edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), 4.0)
    body -= Pos(cx, 0, FLOOR) * Box(s["l"] + 2 * FIT, s["w"] + 2 * FIT, H, align=MIN)
    body -= Pos(_servo_x0() + s["l"] - 6, 0, FLOOR + 6) * Box(14, W + 2, 8, align=MIN)     # bus connector window
    body -= Pos(TORQUE_PIN["x"], 0, 0) * Cylinder(TORQUE_PIN["d"] / 2 - 0.05, 2 * FLOOR)
    return body


def servo_top() -> float:
    return FLOOR + SERVO["bottom"] + SERVO["case_h"]


def servo_dummy() -> Part:
    s = SERVO
    return Pos(_servo_x0() + s["l"] / 2, 0, FLOOR + s["bottom"]) * Box(s["l"], s["w"], s["case_h"], align=MIN) + \
        Pos(0, 0, servo_top()) * Cylinder(s["horn_d"] / 2, s["horn_t"], align=MIN)


def horn_adapter() -> Part:
    """Sits on the horn. Its mating face is up (+Z) at z = NECK + face_t (local frame)."""
    s = SERVO
    p = Cylinder(NECK_R, NECK, align=MIN) + Pos(0, 0, NECK + KX44.face_t) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * male(KX44)
    h = s["horn_sq"] / 2
    for x, y in ((h, h), (h, -h), (-h, h), (-h, -h)):
        p -= Pos(x, y, 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 40)
        p -= Pos(x, y, NECK + KX44.face_t - 3.0) * Cylinder(3.0, 10, align=MIN)
    p -= Cylinder(3.6, 40)                                   # horn centre-screw access
    return p


def adapter_balls() -> Part:
    return Pos(0, 0, NECK + KX44.face_t) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * balls(KX44)


def torque_pin() -> Part:
    return Pos(TORQUE_PIN["x"], 0, -TORQUE_PIN["stick_out"]) * Cylinder(TORQUE_PIN["d"] / 2, TORQUE_PIN["stick_out"] + FLOOR - 0.5, align=MIN)


def stack_height() -> float:
    return servo_top() + SERVO["horn_t"] + NECK + KX44.face_t

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/collision.py ===
```python
"""Collision hulls of the CAD arm for the MuJoCo twin (one convex piece per STL; MuJoCo collides convex hulls).

Frames match the twin: upper pieces in the upper-arm body frame (origin at the yaw joint, arm along -Z), forearm pieces in
the forearm body frame (origin on the elbow axis). CAD +Y = the arm's outboard side (forearm side link). Right-arm copies
are mirrored in Y (the right arm sits at -Y). Units: mm; the twin scales by 0.001.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from build123d import Box, Cylinder, Plane, Pos, Rot, export_stl, mirror

from . import arm, cartridge_m as cm, roll
from .kx import KX74

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "out" / "collision"


def arm_sh_x():
    from ..params import BASELINE
    return BASELINE.arm.shoulder_x * 1000


def arm_sh_y():
    from ..params import BASELINE
    return BASELINE.arm.shoulder_y * 1000


def pieces():
    h = arm.HOUSING
    cart_len = KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]
    y0 = -20.0                                                     # cartridge KX face plane (arm.assembly)
    fy, link_t = arm.FORE_Y, 22.0
    wy = arm.J4310["h"] + 4 + 6
    return {
        "upper": {
            "neck": Pos(0, 0, h["z1"] - 2) * Cylinder(36, 104 - 6 + 2, align=arm.MIN) + Pos(0, 0, -6) * Cylinder(45, 6, align=arm.MIN),
            "housing": Pos(0, 0, (h["z0"] + h["z1"]) / 2) * Box(h["x"], h["y"] + 16, h["z1"] - h["z0"]),
            "elbow_cart": Pos(0, y0 - cart_len / 2, -arm.ELBOW_Z + arm.BELT_C) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(cm.MOTOR["od"] / 2 + 3.3, cart_len),
        },
        "fore": {
            "link": Pos(0, fy, -arm.FORE_L / 2 + 10) * Box(56, link_t, arm.FORE_L - 2 * 38),
            "wrist": Pos(0, arm.WRIST_Y0 - 3, -arm.FORE_L) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(35, arm.WRIST_Y1 - arm.WRIST_Y0 + 3, align=arm.MIN),
        },
        # wrist-pitch body (hand frame = CAD hand frame): palm + KX56 collar; hand (roll) body: gripper, offset +40 mm
        "wristb": {
            "palm": Pos(7, 0, -22) * Box(74, 40, 94),
            "wpcollar": Pos(0, 23, 0) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(43, 19, align=arm.MIN),
        },
        # pitch body ("sh", frame = detach plane at y 0): roll link + roll cartridge
        "sh": {
            "rolllink": Pos(0, 61, 38) * Box(92, 106, 168),
            "rollcart": Pos(-50, roll.ROLL_Y, roll.ROLL_C) * Box(66, 106, 102),
            "cap": Pos(-34, 75, 95) * Box(134, 130, 114),                       # looks-pass shoulder cap
        },
        # roll body (origin on the roll axis): clevis + yaw block + yaw cartridge
        "roll": {
            "yawblock": Pos(-38, 0, (-roll.YAW_DROP + 26) / 2) * Box(172, 92, roll.YAW_DROP + 26),
        },
        # torso body: the torso-side pitch modules (module frame shifted so the detach plane is at the pitch body's y = 0)
        "torso": {
            "pitchmod": Pos(arm_sh_x() - 45, arm_sh_y() - 67.5, 10) * Box(190, 135, 150),
        },
        "hand": {
            "gripbase": Pos(5, 0, 40 - 98) * Box(104, 52, 30),
            "jaws": Pos(-2, 3, 40 - 128) * Box(74, 46, 48),
        },
    }


def export():
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    files = {}
    for body, parts in pieces().items():
        for n, p in parts.items():
            for side, q in (("l", p), ("r", mirror(p, Plane.XZ))):
                f = OUT / f"{body}_{n}_{side}.stl"
                export_stl(q, str(f))
                files[(body, n, side)] = f
    return files


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(len(export()), "collision meshes →", OUT)

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/dock.py ===
```python
"""Self-docking charger, printed parts (queue item 4, loop 26). Validated by rnd/dock_sim.py (100/100 docks).

Dock frame: floor at z = 0, dock axis +X (the robot arrives from +X), V apex at the origin.
- **Two wings**: 10 mm walls, 250 mm faces at ±60° to the axis (a round R 250 base centres itself in the V), 160 mm tall
  (the skirt spans 25–155 mm), with floor tabs for 2 screws each. Printed as mirror-image parts.
- **Contact tower** at the apex: reaches 39 mm toward the robot (the pads on the skirt sit 39 mm from the apex when the base
  is seated). It carries 2 spring leaf contacts (+ / −, at 60 and 100 mm) and a spring-loaded ID/handshake pin (at 40 mm).
  The charger output stays off until the robot answers on the ID pin, so no live contacts at floor level.
- A low-profile lidar target: the V's two faces give a distinctive 120° corner in the 2D scan. An AprilTag goes on the
  tower top for the head camera.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

from build123d import Align, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
V_DEG, FACE_L, WALL_T, H = 60.0, 250.0, 10.0, 160.0     # 10 mm walls: the dock is screwed down, it needs no mass
R_BASE = 250.0
SEAT_X = R_BASE / math.sin(math.radians(V_DEG))           # base centre when seated (289 mm)
PAD_GAP = SEAT_X - R_BASE                                  # apex → pad (39 mm)


def wing(side: int = 1) -> Part:
    a = math.radians(V_DEG)
    w = Pos(FACE_L / 2, side * WALL_T / 2, 0) * Box(FACE_L, WALL_T, H, align=MIN)              # face along +X before rotation
    w += Pos(FACE_L - 20, side * (WALL_T + 15), 0) * (Box(40, 30, 5, align=MIN) - Pos(0, 0, 0) * Cylinder(2.4, 20))   # floor tab
    return Rot(0, 0, side * V_DEG) * w


def tower() -> Part:
    t = Pos(PAD_GAP / 2 - 10, 0, 0) * Box(PAD_GAP + 20, 50, H - 10, align=MIN)
    t -= Pos(PAD_GAP - 2, 0, 60) * Box(8, 12, 30)                                              # leaf-contact pockets (+ at 60, − at 100)
    t -= Pos(PAD_GAP - 2, 0, 100) * Box(8, 12, 30)
    t -= Pos(PAD_GAP - 6, 0, 40) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(3.2, 20)                           # ID/handshake pogo pin
    return t


def parts():
    return [("dock_wing_l", wing(1), "orange"), ("dock_wing_r", wing(-1), "orange"), ("dock_tower", tower(), "dark")]

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/export.py ===
```python
"""Export printable STLs and an exploded render assembly: python -m rnd.parts.export"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from build123d import Pos, Rot, export_stl

from . import belt_stage as bs, cartridge_m as cm, cartridge_s as cs
from .kx import KX44, KX74, collar, hub, pins, retainer, spring

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "out"


def socket(kx, z, explode, x=0.0, locked=False):
    """Hub group flipped to face down onto a cartridge whose mating plane is at z (exploded upward)."""
    f = lambda p, dz=0: Pos(x, 0, z + kx.gap + explode + dz) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * p
    return {"hub": (f(hub(kx), explode * 0.6), "white"), "pins": (f(pins(kx), explode * 0.6), "steel"),
            "spring": (f(spring(kx), explode * 0.6 + 0), "steel"), "retainer": (f(retainer(kx), explode * 0.6), "white"),
            "collar": (f(collar(kx, locked)), "orange")}


def main():
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    printable = {"kx74_male_adapter_gim6010": cm.motor_adapter(), "kx74_hub": hub(KX74), "kx74_collar": Rot(180, 0, 0) * collar(KX74),
                 "kx74_retainer": retainer(KX74), "cartM_can": cm.can(), "coupon_gim6010_boltcircle": cm.coupon_motor(),
                 "kx44_male_horn_adapter": cs.horn_adapter(), "kx44_hub": hub(KX44), "kx44_collar": Rot(180, 0, 0) * collar(KX44),
                 "kx44_retainer": retainer(KX44), "cartS_sleeve": cs.sleeve()}
    printable.update({"htd5m_pulley_20t": bs.pulley(20), "htd5m_pulley_40t": bs.pulley(40), "htd5m_pulley_50t": bs.pulley(50),
                      "htd5m_pulley_60t": bs.pulley(60), "elbow_small_hub_20t": Rot(180, 0, 0) * bs.small_hub()})
    for n, p in printable.items():
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"{n}.stl"))
    e = 34
    ad_z = cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]
    asm = {"m_can": (cm.can(), "white"), "m_motor": (cm.motor_dummy(), "dark"), "m_pin": (cm.torque_pin(), "steel"),
           "m_adapter": (Pos(0, 0, ad_z + e * 0.35) * cm.motor_adapter(), "dark"),
           "m_balls": (Pos(0, 0, ad_z + e * 0.35) * cm.adapter_balls(), "steel")}
    asm.update({f"m_{k}": v for k, v in socket(KX74, ad_z + e * 0.35 + cm.NECK + KX74.face_t, e).items()})
    xs = 120.0
    s_ad = cs.servo_top() + cs.SERVO["horn_t"]
    asm.update({"s_sleeve": (Pos(xs, 0, 0) * cs.sleeve(), "white"), "s_servo": (Pos(xs, 0, 0) * cs.servo_dummy(), "dark"),
                "s_pin": (Pos(xs, 0, 0) * cs.torque_pin(), "steel"),
                "s_adapter": (Pos(xs, 0, s_ad + e * 0.3) * cs.horn_adapter(), "dark"),
                "s_balls": (Pos(xs, 0, s_ad + e * 0.3) * cs.adapter_balls(), "steel")})
    asm.update({f"s_{k}": v for k, v in socket(KX44, s_ad + e * 0.3 + cs.NECK + KX44.face_t, e * 0.8, x=xs).items()})
    for n, (p, _) in asm.items():
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"asm_{n}.stl"))
    (OUT / "asm_colors.txt").write_text("\n".join(f"asm_{n} {c}" for n, (_, c) in asm.items()))
    for f in OUT.glob("*.stl"):
        if f.stem.startswith("asm_") and f.stem not in {f"asm_{n}" for n in asm}:
            f.unlink()
    # elbow belt stage (cartridge above its pulley hub; belt to a 40T joint pulley 70 mm away)
    from .kx import male_mated, balls_mated, collar as col
    hub_g = bs.small_hub()
    zp = hub_g.bounding_box().min.Z + bs.BRG_6004["B"] + 1 + 1.2       # pulley bottom (approx)
    zbelt = zp - 0.2
    el = {"e_hub": (hub_g, "white"), "e_brg": (bs.bearings_dummy(hub_g.bounding_box().min.Z + 2 * bs.BRG_6004["B"] + 1 + 2.4 + 16), "steel"),
          "e_collar": (col(KX74), "orange"), "e_balls": (balls_mated(KX74), "steel"),
          "e_adapter": (Pos(0, 0, KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.motor_adapter(), "dark"),
          "e_motor": (Pos(0, 0, KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.motor_dummy(), "dark"),
          "e_can": (Pos(0, 0, KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.can(), "white"),
          "e_bigpulley": (Pos(70, 0, zp - 1.2) * bs.pulley(40, 16, bore=40), "orange"),
          "e_belt": (Pos(0, 0, zp + 0.5) * bs.belt_loop(31.8, 63.7, 70), "dark")}
    for n, (p, _) in el.items():
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"asm_{n}.stl"))
    (OUT / "asm_elbow.txt").write_text("\n".join(f"asm_{n} {c}" for n, (_, c) in el.items()))
    asm.update(el)
    from . import arm as arm_mod
    ar = arm_mod.assembly()
    for n, p, _ in ar:
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"asm_arm_{n}.stl"))
    (OUT / "asm_arm.txt").write_text("\n".join(f"asm_arm_{n} {c}" for n, _, c in ar))
    printable.update({"arm_upper_bone_unsplit": arm_mod.upper_bone(), "arm_forearm": arm_mod.forearm()})
    for n in ("arm_upper_bone_unsplit", "arm_forearm"):
        export_stl(printable[n], str(OUT / f"{n}.stl"))
    from . import shoulder as sh_mod
    sh = sh_mod.module()
    for n, p, _ in sh:
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"asm_sh_{n}.stl"))
    (OUT / "asm_shoulder.txt").write_text("\n".join(f"asm_sh_{n} {c}" for n, _, c in sh))
    for n, p in {"shoulder_bracket": sh_mod.bracket(), "shoulder_pitch_shaft": sh_mod.pitch_shaft(),
                 "kxv_ferrule_arm_side": sh_mod.ferrule(male=True)}.items():
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"{n}.stl"))
    print("exported", len(printable), "printable parts and", len(asm), "assembly bodies to", OUT)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/hand.py ===
```python
"""Wrist + hand v0: DM-J4310 cartridge (KX56), palm with the wrist-roll cartridge S, parallel gripper, wrist camera.

Hand frame: origin on the wrist-pitch axis (Y), hand centre plane y = 0, hand points along -Z. The wrist-pitch cartridge
sits in the forearm's wrist can; its KX56 hub is on the palm's +Y face. Grasp point at z = -l_hand (130 mm).
- Palm: holds cartridge S #1 (wrist roll) with its shaft along -Z; its KX44 face is at the palm bottom.
- Gripper (on the wrist-roll KX44 hub): SO-100-style: a fixed jaw + a jaw on the horn of cartridge S #2, which lies on
  its side under the base plate. Wrist camera on the base, looking along -Z past the fixed jaw.
DM-J4310 bolt circles are ESTIMATEs (same approach as the GIM: adapter-only dependency, slotted coupon).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

from build123d import Align, Axis, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, fillet

from . import cartridge_s as cs
from .soft import soft_box
from .kx import KX44, KX56, collar, hub_pip as hub, male

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
J4310 = dict(od=57.0, h=46.0, out_holes=6, out_pcd=24.0, rear_holes=4, rear_pcd=46.0)   # PCDs ESTIMATE
WALL, FLOOR, NECK = 2.4, 3.0, 4.0
SLEEVE_TOP = -32.0            # palm: wrist-roll sleeve floor (flipped, horn pointing -Z)
from ..params import BASELINE as _D
L_HAND = _D.arm.l_hand * 1000   # wrist-pitch axis → grasp point (params; loop 10 CAD: 0.13 → 0.15)
WRIST_HUB_Y = 33.0          # KX56 hub face (palm +Y side); the wrist cartridge's male face arrives from +Y


def _rbox(x, y, z, r, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER)):
    b = Box(x, y, z, align=align)
    return fillet(b.edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), min(r, x / 2 - 0.2, y / 2 - 0.2))


# ---------------- cartridge D: DM-J4310 in a can with a KX56 output ----------------
def can_d() -> Part:
    od = J4310["od"] + 2 * WALL + 0.6
    h = J4310["h"] + FLOOR
    p = Cylinder(od / 2, h, align=MIN) - Pos(0, 0, FLOOR) * Cylinder(J4310["od"] / 2 + 0.3, h, align=MIN)
    for k in range(J4310["rear_holes"]):
        a = 2 * math.pi * k / J4310["rear_holes"]
        p -= Pos(J4310["rear_pcd"] / 2 * math.cos(a), J4310["rear_pcd"] / 2 * math.sin(a), 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 10)
    p -= Cylinder(9, 10)
    p -= Pos(od / 2, 0, h / 2 + 3) * Box(10, 14, h - 8)
    p -= Pos(22, 0, 0) * Cylinder(2.45, 2 * FLOOR)                 # Ø5 torque-pin seat
    return p


def adapter_d() -> Part:
    p = Cylinder(25, NECK, align=MIN) + Pos(0, 0, NECK + KX56.face_t) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * male(KX56, bore=6)
    for k in range(J4310["out_holes"]):
        a = 2 * math.pi * k / J4310["out_holes"]
        x, y = J4310["out_pcd"] / 2 * math.cos(a), J4310["out_pcd"] / 2 * math.sin(a)
        p -= Pos(x, y, 0) * Cylinder(1.7, 40)
        p -= Pos(x, y, NECK + KX56.face_t - 3) * Cylinder(3.0, 10, align=MIN)
    return p


def cartridge_d_stack() -> float:
    return FLOOR + J4310["h"] + NECK + KX56.face_t


# ---------------- palm + wrist roll ----------------
def palm() -> Part:
    from .torque import with_pin_slot
    return with_pin_slot(wrist_roll_sleeve(), _palm_body(), 1.95, 2.0)             # loop 25: slotted pad for the wrist-roll cartridge's Ø4 torque pin


def _palm_body() -> Part:
    p = Pos(0, 0, -6) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(30, 40)               # boss around the wrist axis (KX56 hub bolts on +Y)
    zb = SLEEVE_TOP - cs.SERVO["case_h"] - cs.SERVO["bottom"] - cs.FLOOR + 2      # palm bottom ≈ servo top face
    p += Pos(cs.SERVO["l"] / 2 - cs.SERVO["shaft_off"], 0, zb + (-6 - zb) / 2) * soft_box(64, 40, -6 - zb, 16, 10)   # looks pass v2
    # pocket for the flipped wrist-roll sleeve (slides in from the bottom, 2 screws)
    p -= Pos(cs.SERVO["l"] / 2 - cs.SERVO["shaft_off"], 0, zb - 1) * Box(cs.SERVO["l"] + 2 * (cs.FIT + cs.WALL) + 0.6,
                                                                       cs.SERVO["w"] + 2 * (cs.FIT + cs.WALL) + 0.6, SLEEVE_TOP - zb + 1.5, align=MIN)
    return p + wrist_roll_skirt()


def wrist_roll_sleeve() -> Part:
    return Pos(0, 0, SLEEVE_TOP) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cs.sleeve()


def wrist_roll_face_z() -> float:
    return SLEEVE_TOP - cs.stack_height()


# D16 (loop 40, external review finding 5 → A4): the wrist roll no longer hangs the gripper on the servo's KX44 seat.
# With 1 kg in a horizontal hand the seat saw ≈ 0.9 N·m bending (1.3 N·m dynamic) against ≈ 0.6 N·m lift-off
# (P·r_ball/2 = 80 N × 15.5 mm / 2). The gripper now bolts (4 × M3) to a spindle that runs in 2 × 61804 in a skirt under
# the palm; the servo drives it through a two-dog coupling (torque only, floats axially). Cartridge S still slides out of
# the palm (2 screws); tools (suction cup, …) bolt to the spindle flange. The wrist-pitch KX56 stays: its bending lever is
# only the ≈ 40 mm hub offset (≈ 0.9 N·m dynamic vs ≈ 1.5 N·m lift-off).
BRG_WR = dict(d=20.0, D=32.0, B=7.0)          # 61804 (reused: the D13 stages freed the arm's 61804s)
SPINDLE_DROP = 19.3                            # wrist-roll face → gripper base top (was KX44 gap + 12.6 = 13.2)


def _zw():
    return wrist_roll_face_z()


def wrist_roll_skirt() -> Part:
    """Bearing skirt under the palm (part of the palm print): Ø38, seats for the two 61804, lip under the lower one."""
    zf = _zw()
    zP = SLEEVE_TOP - cs.SERVO["case_h"] - cs.SERVO["bottom"] - cs.FLOOR + 2
    sk = Pos(0, 0, zf - 18.5) * Cylinder(19, zP + 1 - (zf - 18.5), align=MIN)
    sk -= Pos(0, 0, zf - 18.5) * Cylinder(13.0, 40, align=MIN)                     # lip / shoulder bore
    sk -= Pos(0, 0, zf - 17.0) * Cylinder(BRG_WR["D"] / 2 + 0.05, 2 * BRG_WR["B"], align=MIN)   # bearing seats
    sk -= Pos(0, 0, zf - 1.5) * Cylinder(11.0, 40, align=MIN)                      # dog adapter clearance up to the servo
    return sk


def wrist_roll_parts():
    zf = _zw()
    zP = SLEEVE_TOP - cs.SERVO["case_h"] - cs.SERVO["bottom"] - cs.FLOOR + 2
    dog = Pos(0, 0, zf) * Cylinder(9.5, zP - 0.5 - zf, align=MIN)                  # on the Ø20 horn (4 × M3)
    dog += Pos(0, 0, zf - 3.5) * Box(4, 18, 3.5, align=MIN)
    sp = Pos(0, 0, zf - 19.3) * Cylinder(BRG_WR["d"] / 2, 19.3 - 0.5 - 3.5 + 3.5, align=MIN)
    sp -= Pos(0, 0, zf - 4.0) * Box(4.8, 30, 10, align=MIN)                         # dog slot (0.4 mm each side)
    sp += Pos(0, 0, zf - 23.3) * Cylinder(20, 4, align=MIN)                          # flange (sits in a 4 mm pocket in the gripper base)
    sp -= Cylinder(4.0, 400)                                                         # wire pass (servo + tactile + ToF)
    brg = lambda z0: Pos(0, 0, z0) * (Cylinder(BRG_WR["D"] / 2, BRG_WR["B"], align=MIN) - Cylinder(BRG_WR["d"] / 2, BRG_WR["B"], align=MIN))
    return [("wr_dog", dog, "dark"), ("wr_spindle", sp, "steel"),
            ("wr_brg_U", brg(zf - 3.0 - BRG_WR["B"]), "steel"), ("wr_brg_L", brg(zf - 17.0), "steel")]


# ---------------- fin-ray finger (TPU) ----------------
def fin_ray(top_z: float, tip_z: float, face_x: float, depth: float, thick: float = 26.0, facing: int = +1, ribs: int = 5) -> Part:
    """Fin-ray finger: triangle in the x-z plane. Vertical contact face at x = face_x (facing ±x toward the object); the back
    face slopes from depth at the top to the tip. Thin TPU walls + cross ribs make the contact face wrap around the
    object (Festo FinRay; UMI gripper). Printed in TPU 95A. thickness along y."""
    from build123d import Polygon, extrude
    s = -facing                                                    # back side direction
    pts = [(face_x, top_z), (face_x + s * depth, top_z), (face_x, tip_z)]
    outer = extrude(Polygon(*pts, align=None), thick)
    w = 1.6
    inner_pts = [(face_x + s * w, top_z - w), (face_x + s * (depth - w * 2.2), top_z - w), (face_x + s * w, tip_z + w * 3.2)]
    inner = extrude(Polygon(*inner_pts, align=None), thick)
    shell = outer - inner
    for k in range(1, ribs + 1):
        z = top_z + (tip_z - top_z) * k / (ribs + 1)
        shell += (Pos((face_x + face_x + s * depth) / 2, z, thick / 2) * Rot(0, 0, s * 12) * Box(depth * 1.2, 1.2, thick)) & inner
    res = Rot(90, 0, 0) * shell                     # extrusion direction depends on the polygon winding: re-centre on y = 0
    bb = res.bounding_box()
    return Pos(0, -(bb.min.Y + bb.max.Y) / 2, 0) * res


# ---------------- gripper ----------------
# Precedent: the SO-100 / LeRobot gripper: one fixed jaw + one jaw driven straight off the servo horn (fewest parts).
# The gripper servo lies on its side under the base plate (shaft along Y, its 24.8 mm width along Z) so the hand stays short.
def gripper_parts(jaw_deg: float = 25.0):
    zb = wrist_roll_face_z() - SPINDLE_DROP      # D16: base plate top = spindle flange top (flange in a 4 mm pocket)
    parts = []
    base = Pos(5, 0, zb - 5) * soft_box(104, 46, 10, 18, 3.5)
    base -= Pos(0, 0, zb - 4) * Cylinder(20.3, 5, align=MIN)                    # spindle flange pocket
    parts.append(("grip_base", base, "white"))
    zs = zb - 10 - 13.0                          # servo shaft height (servo hangs under the base)
    sx = -24.0                                   # shaft x
    servo = Pos(sx + cs.SERVO["l"] / 2 - cs.SERVO["shaft_off"], 0, zs) * Box(cs.SERVO["l"] + 4, 36 + 4, 24.8 + 4)
    parts.append(("grip_servo_sleeve", servo - Pos(sx + cs.SERVO["l"] / 2 - cs.SERVO["shaft_off"], 0, zs) * Box(cs.SERVO["l"], 36, 24.8), "white"))
    parts.append(("grip_servo", Pos(sx + cs.SERVO["l"] / 2 - cs.SERVO["shaft_off"], 0, zs) * Box(cs.SERVO["l"], 36, 24.8), "dark"))
    # moving jaw: lever on the horn (+Y side) → finger under the servo, rotated by jaw_deg about the shaft
    tip = -L_HAND - zs                                           # jaw tip height relative to the shaft
    # loop 31: the lever stopped 5 mm into the base and the mount plate rubbed the servo sleeve through the jaw sweep.
    # Lever now ends 2 mm under the base, outside the base's width; plate centred on the shaft, low enough for ±40°.
    jaw = Pos(0, 27, (tip + 11) / 2) * Box(12, 6, 11 - tip)       # lever on the horn, outside the servo and the base
    jaw += Pos(-6, 10, -25.5) * Box(12, 40, 7)                   # finger mount plate: 22 mm under the shaft and only on its −x side (a point r below the shaft rises to −r·cos θ as the jaw opens under the servo)
    # gripper v2 (loop 16): fin-ray TPU finger (contact face +x) with a tactile pad window
    jaw += Pos(0, 8, 0) * fin_ray(-25.0, tip - 12, 4.5, 18, 28, +1)   # loop 31: top 25 mm under the shaft (was 19.5) so it clears the sleeve at 45°
    parts.append(("jaw_moving", Pos(sx, 0, zs) * Rot(0, -jaw_deg, 0) * jaw, "dark"))
    # fixed jaw: printed stem + fin-ray TPU finger (contact face −x) with the fingertip tactile pad behind its face
    stem = Pos(31, 0, zb - 10) * Box(10, 26, (zb - 10) + L_HAND - 25, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MAX))
    fr = fin_ray(-L_HAND + 25, -L_HAND - 12, 21.0, 18, 28, -1)
    pad = Pos(24.5, 0, -L_HAND + 2) * Box(3, 20, 14)                     # MLX90393 + magnetic elastomer (tactile)
    parts += [("jaw_fixed", stem, "white"), ("finray_fixed", fr, "orange"), ("pad_fixed", pad, "dark")]
    tof = Pos(48, 21, zb - 19) * Box(10, 8, 4)                                  # palm ToF (VL53L4CD) beside the wrist camera, looking down
    parts.append(("palm_tof", tof, "dark"))
    cam = Pos(52.2, 0, zb - 21) * Box(24, 30, 22)                # loop 31: +2.5 mm clear of the fixed fin-ray
    parts.append(("wrist_cam", cam, "dark"))
    return parts


def assembly(jaw_deg: float = 25.0):
    """Hand frame parts: KX56 hub + collar at the wrist, palm, wrist-roll cartridge, gripper."""
    yf = WRIST_HUB_Y
    out = [("wp_hub", Pos(0, yf, 0) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * hub(KX56), "dark"),
           ("wp_collar", Pos(0, yf, 0) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * collar(KX56), "orange"),
           ("palm", palm(), "white"), ("wr_sleeve", wrist_roll_sleeve(), "white")]
    return out + wrist_roll_parts() + gripper_parts(jaw_deg)


def masses(density=1.27):
    printed = palm().volume + sum(p.volume for n, p, c in gripper_parts() if n in ("grip_base", "grip_servo_sleeve", "jaw_moving", "jaw_fixed"))
    return {"palm + gripper printed": printed / 1e6 * density * 0.7,
            "KX56 hub+collar (hand side)": (hub(KX56).volume + collar(KX56).volume) / 1e6 * density * 0.7,
            "D16 wrist-roll spindle + dog (printed) + 2 × 61804": sum(p.volume for n, p, c in wrist_roll_parts() if n in ("wr_dog", "wr_spindle")) / 1e6 * density * 0.9 + 0.016,
            "wrist camera module (ESTIMATE)": 0.05, "TPU pads, rail screws (ESTIMATE)": 0.02}

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/kx.py ===
```python
"""KX joint interface v1: a kinematic seat plus a spring-preloaded breech-lock collar.

Precedents: Maxwell 3-groove kinematic couplings built from a ball on two rods (precision
fixturing); breech-lock and PL lens mounts (twist collar, sprung preload); shaft-mounted
gear reducers held by a torque arm (SEW, e-bike hub motors).

**Load path (v1).** A cartridge carries **torque only**.
- Its output face (male) locks into a **hub** in the driven bone. The hub turns in the
  bone's own bearings, which carry every bending and axial load.
- The motor housing is kept from spinning by a **torque pin** sitting in a radial slot.
  The slot leaves radial and axial freedom, so the cartridge is never over-constrained.

**Parts.**
    male   (on the cartridge output): flange with 3 hardened balls (pressed in) and 3 bayonet lugs
    hub    (in the bone): 3 grooves, each made of two steel dowel pins laid radially
           (a ball on two rods). This is not a printed V-groove: the Hertz contact stress
           on plastic would be about 5x PETG yield (see docs/rnd/kx.md).
    collar (captive on the hub): internal ramped teeth. A 35° twist puts the teeth behind
           the lugs. A spring stack between the collar lip and the hub step sets the preload.
    retainer: screwed under the hub; it keeps the collar and spring captive.

Coordinates in **mated** position: the hub's top face is z = 0; the male arrives from +Z with
its mating face down; the ball centres sit at z = z_ball. All lengths are mm.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

from build123d import Align, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, Sphere, Axis, chamfer

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class KX:
    name: str
    od: float           # male flange OD
    face_t: float       # male flange thickness
    ball_d: float       # hardened steel ball (G25 chrome steel)
    ball_pcd: float
    pin_d: float        # hardened dowel pins forming each groove
    pin_len: float
    lug_reach: float    # lug radial extension beyond od/2
    lug_w: float        # lug tangential width
    lug_t: float        # lug axial thickness (at the back of the flange)
    tooth_t: float      # collar tooth axial thickness
    preload_n: float    # design preload from the spring stack
    beta_deg: float = 20.0      # ball-to-pin contact normal from the face plane. Lower = less lift-out per N·m
                                # -> less preload -> easier twist (sweep in docs/rnd/kx.md: 30° needed 250 N, 20° needs 160 N)
    pin_proud: float = 0.6      # pins stand this far above the hub face (sets the face gap); male face has reliefs
    ball_recess: float = 0.0    # D15: ball centre this far inside the male face (snap past the equator + retaining compound)
    twist_deg: float = 35.0
    ramp_deg: float = 5.0       # below the friction angle (8.5° lubed, 14° dry PETG) -> self-locking. Loop 40: 6 → 5° (twist p95 sat at the 3 N·m rule with D15)
    clearance: float = 0.3
    spring_t: float = 2.0       # D15 (loop 40): gap between the hub step and the collar lip; 6 coil springs Ø4 sit in 3 mm
                                # pockets in the step (installed 5 mm). Was 1.2 mm with a 1 mm-wide annulus: no real spring fits
    stroke: float = 1.0         # teeth sit this far below the lug backs: the last ~14° of twist lifts
                                # the collar by `stroke`, compressing the spring -> preload (k ≈ preload / 1.5 mm)

    # ---- derived geometry ----
    @property
    def r_ball(self): return self.ball_pcd / 2

    @property
    def pin_z(self): return -self.pin_d / 2 + self.pin_proud

    @property
    def z_ball(self):
        return self.pin_z + (self.ball_d + self.pin_d) / 2 * math.sin(math.radians(self.beta_deg))

    @property
    def pin_sep(self):                                         # pin centre spacing
        return (self.ball_d + self.pin_d) * math.cos(math.radians(self.beta_deg))

    @property
    def gap(self): return self.z_ball - self.ball_recess        # male face height above hub face

    @property
    def lug_back_z(self): return self.gap + self.face_t        # back of the male flange / lugs (mated)

    @property
    def r_collar_in(self): return self.od / 2 + self.lug_reach + self.clearance + 0.4

    @property
    def r_hub(self): return self.r_collar_in - 0.3

    @property
    def r_collar_out(self): return self.r_collar_in + 5.5

    @property
    def r_tooth_in(self): return self.od / 2 + self.clearance + 0.2   # teeth must clear the flange while inserting


# Cartridge M (GIM6010-8, DM-J4310). Cartridge S (STS3215).
# D15 (loop 40, external review finding 2): pins 1 mm prouder and balls 1 mm deeper in the male face (captured past
# their equator), so the face gap is unchanged.
KX74 = KX("KX74", od=74, face_t=7, ball_d=10.0, ball_pcd=58, pin_d=5.0, pin_len=14,
          lug_reach=6, lug_w=14, lug_t=3.0, tooth_t=2.4, preload_n=160, pin_proud=1.6, ball_recess=1.0)
# DM-J4310 (Ø57, 7 N·m peak): KX74 is oversized for the wrist and upper-arm yaw (loop 4 mass pass)
KX56 = KX("KX56", od=56, face_t=6, ball_d=8.0, ball_pcd=43, pin_d=4.0, pin_len=11,
          lug_reach=5, lug_w=11, lug_t=2.5, tooth_t=2.2, preload_n=140, pin_proud=1.4, ball_recess=0.8)
KX44 = KX("KX44", od=44, face_t=5, ball_d=6.0, ball_pcd=31, pin_d=3.0, pin_len=8,
          lug_reach=4, lug_w=9, lug_t=2.5, tooth_t=2.0, preload_n=80, pin_proud=1.2, ball_recess=0.6)
BALL_ANG = (90, 210, 330)
LUG_ANG = (30, 150, 270)
HUB_STEP = 6.0       # hub keeps r_hub from z=0 to -HUB_STEP, then steps in by STEP_IN
STEP_IN = 5.5        # D15: 1.5 → 5.5, room for the spring pockets
SPRING_D, SPRING_POCKET = 4.4, 3.0
SPRING_ANG = (0, 60, 120, 180, 240, 300)   # between the pin pairs (90/210/330) and the retainer screws (30/150/270)
LIP_T = 2.0


def _at(r, ang):
    return Pos(r * math.cos(math.radians(ang)), r * math.sin(math.radians(ang)), 0)


def male(kx: KX, bore: float = 0.0) -> Part:
    """Male face in its own frame: z in [0, face_t], mating face at z = 0 (normal -Z), lugs at the back."""
    p = Cylinder(kx.od / 2, kx.face_t, align=MIN)
    p = chamfer(p.edges().group_by(Axis.Z)[0], 0.6)       # lead-in on the mating edge
    for a in LUG_ANG:
        # back face ramped like the collar teeth -> face contact, not an edge
        p += _at(kx.od / 2 + kx.lug_reach / 2 - 1, a) * Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos(0, 0, kx.face_t) * Rot(-kx.ramp_deg, 0, 0) * \
            Box(kx.lug_reach + 2, kx.lug_w, kx.lug_t, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MAX))
    for a in BALL_ANG:                                    # D15: pockets past the equator (ball centre ball_recess inside the face)
        p -= _at(kx.r_ball, a) * Pos(0, 0, kx.ball_recess) * Sphere(kx.ball_d / 2 - 0.05)
        # relief so the proud pins clear the face
        p -= _at(kx.r_ball, a) * Rot(0, 0, a) * Box(kx.pin_len + 2, kx.pin_sep + kx.pin_d + 2, 2 * (kx.pin_proud + 0.5))
    if bore:
        p -= Cylinder(bore / 2, 100)
    return p


def balls(kx: KX) -> Part:
    out = None
    for a in BALL_ANG:
        b = _at(kx.r_ball, a) * Sphere(kx.ball_d / 2)
        out = b if out is None else out + b
    return out


def _pin(kx: KX, a: float, side: int) -> Part:
    """One dowel pin, radial, offset tangentially by ±pin_sep/2 (mated frame)."""
    return Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos(kx.r_ball, side * kx.pin_sep / 2, kx.pin_z) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * \
        Cylinder(kx.pin_d / 2, kx.pin_len)


def pins(kx: KX) -> Part:
    out = None
    for a in BALL_ANG:
        for s in (-1, 1):
            out = _pin(kx, a, s) if out is None else out + _pin(kx, a, s)
    return out


def hub(kx: KX, depth: float = None, bore: float = 0.0, screws: bool = True) -> Part:
    """Female hub (top of a bone's driven member). Face at z = 0, body down to -depth."""
    depth = depth or HUB_STEP + LIP_T + kx.spring_t + 1.0
    p = Pos(0, 0, -HUB_STEP) * Cylinder(kx.r_hub, HUB_STEP, align=MIN)
    p += Pos(0, 0, -depth) * Cylinder(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN, depth - HUB_STEP, align=MIN)
    for a in BALL_ANG:
        # pin bores (pressed in radially from the outside; the access hole is the same size)
        for s in (-1, 1):
            p -= Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos((kx.r_ball - kx.pin_len / 2 + kx.r_hub + 2) / 2, s * kx.pin_sep / 2, kx.pin_z) * \
                Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(kx.pin_d / 2, kx.r_hub + 2 - (kx.r_ball - kx.pin_len / 2))
        # relief channel: exposes the pin contact lines; deeper slot clears the ball's bottom
        p -= Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos(kx.r_ball, 0, 0) * Box(kx.pin_len + 1, kx.pin_sep, -kx.pin_z * 2)
        p -= Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos(kx.r_ball, 0, 0) * Box(kx.ball_d, kx.pin_sep - kx.pin_d, (kx.ball_d / 2 - kx.z_ball + 0.8) * 2)
    # 3 × M3 holes for the retainer (screwed version only)
    for a in LUG_ANG if screws else ():
        p -= _at(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN - 3.5, a) * Pos(0, 0, -depth) * Cylinder(1.25, 8, align=MIN)
    # D15: 6 spring pockets in the step face (springs push the collar lip down → teeth onto the lug backs → preload)
    for a in SPRING_ANG:
        p -= _at(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN / 2, a) * Pos(0, 0, -HUB_STEP) * Cylinder(SPRING_D / 2, SPRING_POCKET, align=MIN)
    if bore:
        p -= Cylinder(bore / 2, 100)
    return p


def retainer(kx: KX, depth: float = None) -> Part:
    depth = depth or HUB_STEP + LIP_T + kx.spring_t + 1.0
    r = Pos(0, 0, -depth - 2) * (Cylinder(kx.r_collar_in + 1.5, 2, align=MIN) - Cylinder(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN - 7, 2, align=MIN))
    for a in LUG_ANG:
        r -= _at(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN - 3.5, a) * Pos(0, 0, -depth - 2) * Cylinder(1.7, 2, align=MIN)
    return r


def hub_pip(kx: KX, depth: float = None, bore: float = 0.0) -> Part:
    """D15 (loop 40): the socket as built = hub + screwed retainer (3 × M3 into heat-set inserts), shown as one solid
    for geometry checks. The name is kept for callers.

    Loop 10's print-in-place version (hub + retainer in one print, collar printed around it, pauses for the pins and the
    spring) is retired for the first build (external review finding 2): its 1 mm spring annulus held no real spring,
    the pauses were listed in the wrong order (the lip layer prints before the pin layer: the hub is printed face-up),
    and the hub step had to bridge over a loose spring. It stays a coupon experiment (C1) for a later build-time cut.

    Assembly: press the 6 dowel pins in radially; put 6 springs in the step pockets; slide the collar up from below;
    screw the retainer on. ≈ 8 min per socket (ESTIMATE)."""
    depth = depth or HUB_STEP + LIP_T + kx.spring_t + 1.0
    core = Pos(0, 0, -depth - 2) * Cylinder(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN - 7, 2, align=MIN)
    return hub(kx, depth, bore, screws=True) + retainer(kx, depth) + core - (Cylinder(bore / 2, 400) if bore else Pos(0, 0, 1000) * Cylinder(1, 1))


def spring(kx: KX) -> Part:
    """D15: the 6 coil springs (Ø4, installed 5 mm: 3 mm in the pockets + the 2 mm gap to the collar lip)."""
    out = None
    for a in SPRING_ANG:
        c = _at(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN / 2, a) * Pos(0, 0, -HUB_STEP - kx.spring_t) * Cylinder(1.9, kx.spring_t + SPRING_POCKET - 0.2, align=MIN)
        out = c if out is None else out + c
    return out


def collar(kx: KX, locked: bool = True) -> Part:
    """Collar in the mated frame. Unlocked = rotated +twist_deg (the teeth sit between the lugs)."""
    z0 = -HUB_STEP - kx.spring_t - LIP_T
    top = kx.lug_back_z + kx.tooth_t
    c = Pos(0, 0, z0) * (Cylinder(kx.r_collar_out, top - z0, align=MIN) - Cylinder(kx.r_collar_in, top - z0, align=MIN))
    c += Pos(0, 0, z0) * (Cylinder(kx.r_collar_in + 0.1, LIP_T, align=MIN) - Cylinder(kx.r_hub - STEP_IN + 0.3, LIP_T, align=MIN))
    reach = kx.r_collar_in - kx.r_tooth_in
    for a in LUG_ANG:
        # tooth underside slopes by ramp_deg: its leading edge (toward the lug) is higher
        t = Pos(0, 0, kx.lug_back_z - kx.stroke) * Rot(-kx.ramp_deg, 0, 0) * Box(reach + 1.0, kx.lug_w - 2, kx.tooth_t, align=MIN)
        c += Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos(kx.r_tooth_in + (reach + 1.0) / 2, 0, 0) * t
    for k in range(24):                                   # grip flutes
        c -= _at(kx.r_collar_out + 0.5, k * 15) * Pos(0, 0, z0) * Cylinder(1.1, top - z0, align=MIN)
    for a in (LUG_ANG[0] + 60, LUG_ANG[1] + 60):          # two thumb tabs for leverage
        c += Rot(0, 0, a) * Pos(kx.r_collar_out + 3, 0, z0) * Box(9, 10, top - z0 - 2, align=MIN)
    return c if locked else Rot(0, 0, kx.twist_deg) * c


def male_mated(kx: KX, bore: float = 0.0) -> Part:
    return Pos(0, 0, kx.gap) * male(kx, bore)


def balls_mated(kx: KX) -> Part:
    return Pos(0, 0, kx.gap) * balls(kx)

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/robot.py ===
```python
"""Whole-robot assembly for renders: CAD arms + shoulder modules on placeholder base/spine/torso/head.

Placeholders are marked by name ("ph_*") until those modules are designed. Frames follow the twin (x forward, z up,
right arm at -y). Units mm. python -m rnd.parts.robot [lift_m]
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from build123d import Align, Axis, Box, Cylinder, Plane, Pos, Rot, export_stl, fillet, mirror

from ..params import BASELINE
from . import arm, base, roll, shoulder, upper

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "out"


def _rbox(x, y, z, r):
    return fillet(Box(x, y, z).edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), r)


def omni_wheel():
    """6" omni placeholder: hub + 14 rollers on the rim (axis along Y)."""
    w = Cylinder(60, 36) - Cylinder(14, 36)
    for k in range(14):
        a = 2 * math.pi * k / 14
        w += Pos(66 * math.cos(a), 66 * math.sin(a), 0) * Rot(0, 0, math.degrees(a) + 90) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(10, 26)
    return Rot(90, 0, 0) * w


def build(lift=None, poses=None):
    d = BASELINE
    b, u, a = d.base, d.upper, d.arm
    lift_mm = (lift if lift is not None else 0.75) * 1000
    out = []
    # ---- base (CAD: rnd/parts/base.py) ----
    out += base.module()
    # ---- mast, lift, carriage, torso, head (CAD: rnd/parts/upper.py) ----
    out += upper.module(lift_mm / 1000)
    # ---- arms: shoulder pitch module (CAD) + roll stage (placeholder) + CAD arm ----
    poses = poses or {"l": dict(pitch=-15, elbow=-70, wrist=-20), "r": dict(pitch=-70, elbow=-45, wrist=-25)}
    for side, s in (("l", 1), ("r", -1)):
        P = poses[side]
        sh = Pos(a.shoulder_x * 1000, s * a.shoulder_y * 1000, lift_mm)
        mir = (lambda p: p) if s == 1 else (lambda p: mirror(p, Plane.XZ))
        for n, p, c in shoulder.module():
            out.append((f"{side}_sh_{n}", sh * mir(Pos(0, -(shoulder.Y_FER + 18), 0) * p), c))   # detach plane at the pitch body origin
        piv = sh * Rot(0, P["pitch"], 0)
        for n, p, c in roll.module():
            out.append((f"{side}_roll_{n}", piv * mir(p), c))
        for n, p, c in arm.assembly(elbow_deg=P["elbow"], wrist_deg=P["wrist"]):
            out.append((f"{side}_arm_{n}", piv * mir(Pos(0, roll.ROLL_Y, -roll.YAW_DROP) * p), c))
    return out


def export(lift=None, name="robot", poses=None):
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    parts = build(lift, poses)
    for n, p, _ in parts:
        export_stl(p, str(OUT / f"asm_{name}_{n}.stl"))
    (OUT / f"asm_{name}.txt").write_text("\n".join(f"asm_{name}_{n} {c}" for n, _, c in parts))
    return OUT / f"asm_{name}.txt"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(export(float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None))

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/roll.py ===
```python
"""Arm-side shoulder: KX-V male ferrule → roll link (roll stage 18T:72T) → clevis → yaw stage (20T:40T) → upper-arm flange.

Frame = the twin's pitch body for the LEFT arm (outboard +Y): pitch axis = Y through the origin, detach plane at y = 0.
Roll axis = X through (y = ROLL_Y, z = 0). Yaw axis = Z through (x = 0, y = ROLL_Y), upper-arm flange top at z = -YAW_DROP.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

from build123d import Align, Axis, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, fillet

from . import cartridge_m as cm
from .belt_stage import pulley, small_hub
from .kx import KX74, collar
from .shoulder import ferrule

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
from ..params import BASELINE as _D
from .belt_stage import V2 as _V2, small_stage_v2
ROLL_Y = _D.arm.roll_offset_y * 1000                 # loop 10: 50 → 75 (the Ø114 roll pulley); loop 40: 83 (the 80T pulley clears the ferrule by 2 mm)
# loop 40 (D13 migration of roll + yaw; yaw ±150° restored with a clock-spring cassette):
#  - roll belt centre 90 → 102: the D13 yaw stage is 11 mm taller, so the yaw cartridge (roll output) reaches 48 mm from the
#    roll axis; the static roll collar/can must sit ≥ 2 mm outside that over the whole roll sweep (rnd/spacing.py swept).
#    Roll belt → stock HTD 475-5M.
#  - roll 72T → 80T (ratio 4.0 → 4.44): the D13 stages put 0.16 kg more on the shoulder, and the whole-workspace roll
#    margin (rated / p95, 1 kg, yaw ±150°) fell to 1.49×; 80T restores ≈ 1.6× with no motor change.
ROLL_TEETH = 80
ROLL_C, YAW_C = 103.3, 80.0          # 80T:18T roll on a stock 475 mm belt
_HUB_H = _V2["hub_h"]                                  # 12.2
JA, JB = _V2["jA"], _V2["jB"]                          # D13 bearing spans (stage frame, below the KX face: negative z)
PUL_C = -_V2["belt_c"]                                 # 40.2: small-pulley belt centre below the KX face


def _stage(T, teeth=20):
    """Cartridge M + collar + D13 small stage v2 in a stage frame (KX face z = 0, cartridge +Z), mapped by T."""
    zc = KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]
    out = [("can", T * Pos(0, 0, zc) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.can(), "white"),
           ("motor", T * Pos(0, 0, zc) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.motor_dummy(), "dark"),
           ("collar", T * collar(KX74), "orange")]
    return out + [(n, T * pp, c) for n, pp, c in small_stage_v2(teeth)]


def _fl(b, r, dims):
    r = min(r, min(dims) / 2 - 0.3)
    return fillet(b.edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), r) if r > 0.5 else b


def _plate_x(x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, r=10):
    return Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * _fl(Box(z1 - z0, y1 - y0, x1 - x0), r, (z1 - z0, y1 - y0))
    return Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * fillet(Box(z1 - z0, y1 - y0, x1 - x0).edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), r)


def _plate_z(x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, r=10):
    return Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * _fl(Box(x1 - x0, y1 - y0, z1 - z0), r, (x1 - x0, y1 - y0))


# roll stage: cartridge behind (−X), KX face at x = XF; 6 mm walls with the D13 bearings flush on the pulley side
XF = -PUL_C                                            # stage z → world x = XF − z; belt plane x = 0
W1 = (XF - JA[0] - 6.0, XF - JA[0])                    # inner wall (bearing A at x −15.5 … −10.5, flush at −10.5)
W2 = (XF - JB[1], XF - JB[1] + 6.0)                    # outer wall (bearing B at x 17.5 … 22.5)
ROLL_T = Pos(XF, ROLL_Y, ROLL_C) * Rot(0, -90, 0)        # stage +Z → world −X
HEX_AF = 34.0                                          # shaft hex under the split pulleys (corners Ø39.3: passes the 61808 bore)
CAV_H = 9.5                                            # clock-spring cavity height (D14 ribbon 7.8 mm + clearance)
ROLL_SPR = (22.0, 38.0)                                # roll clock spring r_in, r_out
YAW_SPR = (22.5, 40.0)                                 # yaw clock spring (inner wall = the 40T pulley's hub)


def _hex(af, h):
    from build123d import RegularPolygon, extrude
    return extrude(RegularPolygon(af / math.sqrt(3), 6), h)


def roll_link() -> Part:
    p = Pos(0, 8, 0) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(45, 4, align=MIN)            # boss behind the ferrule (y 8..12)
    for x0, x1 in (W1, W2):
        w = _plate_x(x0, x1, 8, ROLL_Y + 40, -22, ROLL_C + 32, 14)
        w -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(26, 100)                # 61808 seat, roll shaft
        w -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, ROLL_C) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(19.1, 100)         # eccentric-bushing seat, D13 stage
        p += w
    p += _plate_x(W1[0], W2[1], 8, 12, 46, ROLL_C + 32, 4)                       # web joining the walls to the boss, above the roll pulley
    return p + roll_torque_fork() + roll_spring_static()


ZC = KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]      # KX face → can rear


def roll_torque_fork() -> Part:
    """Loop 22: torque reaction for the roll cartridge. Its pin sits 30 mm above the cartridge axis (z = ROLL_C + 30) and
    sticks out of the can rear (−X). The inner wall rises to z 147; a 6 mm plate runs over the can (85 mm wide in Y:
    ≈ 3.8 MPa at 367 N) to a 7 mm fork plate with a slot open downward (toward the cartridge axis). Everything stays
    > 105 mm from the roll axis, outside the swept envelope of the clevis and yaw block."""
    tp = cm.TORQUE_PIN
    x_rear, zp = XF - ZC, ROLL_C + tp["r"]
    y0, y1 = 30.0, ROLL_Y + 40
    zt = ROLL_C + 54                                                        # 144 at ROLL_C 90: clears the KX collar (r 49.2)
    riser = Pos((W1[0] + W1[1]) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (ROLL_C + 30 + zt + 3) / 2) * Box(W1[1] - W1[0], y1 - y0, zt + 3 - (ROLL_C + 30))
    top = Pos((x_rear - 9 + W1[1]) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, zt) * Box(W1[1] - (x_rear - 9), y1 - y0, 6)
    fork = Pos(x_rear - 5.5, ROLL_Y, (zp - 9 + zt) / 2) * Box(7, 48, zt - (zp - 9))
    fork -= Pos(x_rear - 5.5, ROLL_Y, zp - 9 + 7) * Box(8, tp["d"] + 0.4, 14 + 0.01)         # slot: open bottom, closed 5 mm above the pin
    return riser + top + fork


def roll_shaft() -> Part:
    """Loop 40 (R14): Ø40 tube, one piece, no integral pulley: it slides in from −X through the −X clevis arm, wall W1's
    61808, the 72T pulley (hex AF 34), wall W2's 61808 and the +X clevis arm, and carries the roll clock-spring hub on
    its +X end."""
    x0, x1 = -29.0, ROLL_SPR_X[1] - 0.5
    s = Cylinder(20, x1 - x0, align=MIN) - Cylinder(14, x1 - x0, align=MIN)
    s -= Pos(0, 0, -10 - x0) * (Cylinder(21, 20, align=MIN) - _hex(HEX_AF, 20))
    return Pos(x0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * s


def roll_pulley() -> Part:
    """80T HTD 5M, 16 wide, hex bore (AF 34.4), printed PETG; located between the inner races by 2 printed shims."""
    p = pulley(ROLL_TEETH, 16, flange=False) - _hex(HEX_AF + 0.4, 16)
    r_rim = ROLL_TEETH * 5.0 / math.pi / 2 - 0.57 - 2.06 - 5.0
    for z in (0.0, 10.5):                                                    # lightening: 5 mm web between a Ø56 hub and a 5 mm rim
        p -= Pos(0, 0, z) * (Cylinder(r_rim, 5.5, align=MIN) - Cylinder(28, 5.5, align=MIN))
    return Pos(-8, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * p


# roll clock spring (A3): outboard of the +X clevis arm. The rotating hub (disc + sleeve) screws to the clevis arm; the
# static ring + cover hang on a bar that runs inboard (−Y, at z ≈ 0: a direction the roll output never sweeps) to the
# roll-link boss. The ribbon's static end leaves through the ring toward the detach plug; its rotating end runs down a
# groove in the clevis arm to the yaw cassette.
ROLL_SPR_X = (W2[1] + 12 + 1.5, W2[1] + 12 + 1.5 + CAV_H)   # cavity x-span (37.0 … 46.5)


def roll_spring_hub() -> Part:
    x0, x1 = ROLL_SPR_X
    h = Pos(x0 - 1.5, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * (Cylinder(ROLL_SPR[1] - 0.8, 1.5, align=MIN) - Cylinder(20.1, 1.5, align=MIN))
    h += Pos(x0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * (Cylinder(ROLL_SPR[0], x1 - x0 - 0.5, align=MIN) - Cylinder(20.1, x1 - x0, align=MIN))
    return h


def roll_spring_static() -> Part:
    x0, x1 = ROLL_SPR_X
    r0, r1 = ROLL_SPR
    ring = Pos(x0 + 0.5, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * (Cylinder(r1 + 2, x1 - x0 + 1.6 - 0.5, align=MIN) - Cylinder(r1, x1 - x0 - 0.5, align=MIN))
    ring -= Pos(x1 - 1, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(r0 + 0.5, 5, align=MIN)      # cover clears the rotating sleeve
    ring -= Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, ROLL_Y, r1 + 1) * Box(CAV_H - 1, 10, 4)                      # ribbon exit (top)
    bar = Pos((x0 + 1 + x1 + 1.6) / 2, (12 + ROLL_Y - r1) / 2, 0) * Box(x1 + 1.6 - x0 - 1, ROLL_Y - r1 - 12 + 1, 14)
    return ring + bar


# yaw stage: yaw axis Z at (0, ROLL_Y); belt plane horizontal; cartridge above its D13 stage at x = -YAW_C.
# Loop 40: top plate 3 mm under the roll pulley; 6 mm plates with the D13 bearings flush on the pulley side. The yaw clock
# spring sits between the 40T pulley and the bottom plate (cavity 9.5 mm), so the drop added is the D13 stage's length
# and the 80T roll pulley: yaw_drop 100 → 113.0 mm.
_R_ROLL = ROLL_TEETH * 5.0 / math.pi / 2 - 0.5715       # roll pulley outer radius
P1 = (-(_R_ROLL + 3.0) - 6.0, -(_R_ROLL + 3.0))        # top plate z-span (bearing A flush on its underside)
ZF = P1[0] - JA[0]                     # KX face of the yaw cartridge (−36.0)
YAW_T = Pos(-YAW_C, ROLL_Y, ZF)
P2 = (ZF + JB[1] - 6.0, ZF + JB[1])    # bottom plate (−99.7 … −93.7), bearing B flush on its top
YAW_BELT = ZF - PUL_C                  # −76.2
YAW_DROP = round(-(P2[0] - 2.3 - 5.0), 1)   # flange: 5 mm, 2.3 mm under the bottom plate (R15 moving ≥ 2; 113.4 = params.arm.yaw_drop)


YAW_WALL_Y = 44.0                      # side-wall centre offset from the yaw axis (plates span ±46)


def clevis_and_yaw_block() -> Part:
    p = None
    for z0, z1 in (P1, P2):
        pl = _plate_z(-YAW_C - 48, 48, ROLL_Y - 46, ROLL_Y + 46, z0, z1, 16)
        pl -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Cylinder(26, 400)                                # 61808 seat, yaw shaft
        pl -= Pos(-YAW_C, ROLL_Y, 0) * Cylinder(19.1, 400)                        # eccentric-bushing seat, small hub
        p = pl if p is None else p + pl
    # confidence campaign (FEA): the bottom plate was a free solid. Four Ø14 posts outside the belt loop tie it to the top plate
    # (M5 through-bolts; the belt and both pulleys go in before the bottom plate)
    for xx, yy in ((-YAW_C / 2, ROLL_Y - 38), (-YAW_C / 2, ROLL_Y + 38), (-YAW_C - 24, ROLL_Y - 38), (-YAW_C - 24, ROLL_Y + 38)):
        p += Pos(xx, yy, P2[1]) * (Cylinder(7, P1[0] - P2[1], align=MIN) - Cylinder(2.75, P1[0] - P2[1], align=MIN))
    # loop 40 (A8, rnd/stiffness.py): two plates on four posts tilted the yaw seats ≈ 22 mrad (P2) under the arm moment
    # (≈ 17 mm at the hand). 4 mm side walls along both long edges close the block into a box section (belt clear by 9 mm;
    # the +X end stays open for the ribbon). The walls belong to the top half; the bottom plate still bolts on last.
    for yy in (ROLL_Y + YAW_WALL_Y, ROLL_Y - YAW_WALL_Y):
        p += Pos((-112 + 32) / 2, yy, (P2[1] + P1[0]) / 2) * Box(144, 4, P1[0] - P2[1])
    p += yaw_torque_fork()
    t = math.radians(STOP_PIN_AZ)
    p -= Pos(STOP_PIN_R * math.cos(t), ROLL_Y + STOP_PIN_R * math.sin(t), P2[0]) * Cylinder(2.95, 5, align=MIN)   # loop 34: press-fit seat for the stop pin
    for x0, x1 in ((W1[0] - 12, W1[0] - 2), (W2[1] + 2, W2[1] + 12)):            # clevis arms outside the walls
        arm = _plate_x(x0, x1, ROLL_Y - 26, ROLL_Y + 26, P1[0], 26, 10)           # arms end on the top plate (yaw pulley below is clear)
        arm -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(20.2, 100)           # clamps on the roll shaft ends
        arm -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Cylinder(22.0, 400)                            # loop 31/36: 2 mm clear of the rotating Ø40 yaw shaft (R15)
        p += arm
    return p


def yaw_torque_fork() -> Part:
    """Loop 22: torque reaction for the yaw cartridge (axis Z at x = −YAW_C). Its pin sits 30 mm toward the yaw axis
    (x = −YAW_C + 30) above the can rear (z = ZF + ZC). A 7 mm fork plate, open toward the cartridge axis (−X), is fused to
    the inboard clevis arm; the roll-shaft envelope is cut as in the arm. In-plane bending ≈ 2.8 MPa at 367 N."""
    tp = cm.TORQUE_PIN
    xp, z_rear = -YAW_C + tp["r"], ZF + ZC
    xe = W1[0] - 7
    f = Pos((xp - 9 + xe) / 2, ROLL_Y, z_rear + 5.5) * Box(xe - (xp - 9), 48, 7)
    f -= Pos(xp - 9 + 7, ROLL_Y, z_rear + 5.5) * Box(14 + 0.01, tp["d"] + 0.4, 8)             # open slot toward −X, closed 5 mm past the pin
    f -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(20.2, 60)                 # roll-shaft envelope, only where the shaft is (x ±30)
    return f


def yaw_shaft() -> Part:
    """Loop 40 (R14): Ø40 tube + bottom flange, one piece; the 40T pulley is separate (hex AF 34). Inserted from below
    through the bottom plate's 61808, the static spring ring, the pulley and the top plate's 61808. The upper bone bolts
    to the flange inside the stop slot (6 × M4 at r 28), so no torque crosses the slot."""
    h = P1[1] + 2 - (-YAW_DROP)
    s = Pos(0, ROLL_Y, -YAW_DROP) * (Cylinder(20, h, align=MIN) - Cylinder(14, 200, align=MIN))
    z0 = P2[1]
    s -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, z0) * (Cylinder(21, YAW_BELT + 8 - z0, align=MIN) - _hex(HEX_AF, YAW_BELT + 8 - z0))
    s += Pos(0, ROLL_Y, -YAW_DROP) * (Cylinder(45, 5, align=MIN) - Cylinder(14, 5, align=MIN))   # flange → upper bone
    # hard-stop slot (blind, 3.5 mm deep in the top of the flange) for the fixed Ø6 stop pin: software ±150°, hard ±155°
    s -= Pos(0, ROLL_Y, -YAW_DROP + 1.5) * _arc_slot(STOP_PIN_R, 3.3, STOP_PIN_AZ, YAW_STOP_DEG, 4.0)
    return s


def _arc_slot(r, hw, az, half, h):
    """Annular slot of half-width hw at radius r, ±half degrees about azimuth az (round ends), height h, from z = 0."""
    from build123d import Polygon, extrude
    ring = Cylinder(r + hw, h, align=MIN) - Cylinder(r - hw, h, align=MIN)
    keep = [(0.0, 0.0)] + [(3 * r * math.cos(math.radians(az + 180 + a)), 3 * r * math.sin(math.radians(az + 180 + a))) for a in range(-(180 - half), 180 - half + 1, 5)]
    gap = extrude(Polygon(*keep, align=None), h)                      # the solid sector the slot must NOT cut (around az + 180)
    ring -= gap
    for a in (-half, half):
        t = math.radians(az + a)
        ring += Pos(r * math.cos(t), r * math.sin(t), 0) * Cylinder(hw, h, align=MIN)
    return ring


def yaw_pulley() -> Part:
    """40T HTD 5M on the belt plane, hex bore, with a Ø45 hub below it that is the clock spring's rotating inner wall
    (r 22.5 ≥ 10 × the Ø2.2 USB cable's OD); the hub stands on bearing B's inner race."""
    p = Pos(0, 0, YAW_BELT - 8) * pulley(40, 16, flange=False)
    p += Pos(0, 0, P2[1]) * Cylinder(21.6, 1.0, align=MIN) + Pos(0, 0, P2[1] + 1.0) * Cylinder(YAW_SPR[0], YAW_BELT - 8 - P2[1] - 1.0, align=MIN)
    p -= Pos(0, 0, P2[1]) * _hex(HEX_AF + 0.4, 40)
    return Pos(0, ROLL_Y, 0) * p


def yaw_spring_ring() -> Part:
    """Static outer wall of the yaw clock spring, screwed to the bottom plate; ribbon exit toward +X (cable comes down
    the +X clevis arm and in between the plates)."""
    r1 = YAW_SPR[1]
    top = YAW_BELT - 8 - 0.8
    g = Pos(0, ROLL_Y, P2[1]) * (Cylinder(r1 + 2, top - P2[1], align=MIN) - Cylinder(r1, top - P2[1], align=MIN))
    g -= Pos(r1 + 1, ROLL_Y, P2[1] + 0.5) * Box(4, 10, CAV_H, align=MIN)
    return g


YAW_STOP_DEG = 155                    # hard stops (software limit ±150°, rnd/twin.py JR); loop 40, was ±75°
STOP_PIN_R, STOP_PIN_AZ = 38.0, 180.0 # stop pin radius and azimuth at yaw 0 (under the bottom plate, toward −X)


def yaw_stop_pin() -> Part:
    """Ø6 × 10 hardened dowel pressed 5 mm into the bottom yaw plate, protruding 1.9 + 3 mm into the flange's blind arc slot.
    A fault drive into the stop at the yaw's 22 N·m peak puts ≈ 580 N on the pin: ≈ 32 MPa bearing over 3 mm of printed
    flange, fault-only (software limit ±150°); a 3 mm stub sees ≈ 1.7 N·m bending, ≈ 80 MPa in the pin."""
    t = math.radians(STOP_PIN_AZ)
    return Pos(STOP_PIN_R * math.cos(t), ROLL_Y + STOP_PIN_R * math.sin(t), P2[0] + 5) * Cylinder(3, 9.9, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MAX))


def shoulder_cap() -> Part:
    """Looks pass: rounded cap over the roll cartridge and the top of the roll link (pitch body; open underneath).
    It stays clear of everything that rolls: the clevis stays within 37 mm of the roll axis and the cap starts 62 mm above it."""
    from .soft import soft_shell
    dz = ROLL_C - 90                                                          # loop 40: roll belt centre 90 → 102
    y1 = ROLL_Y + 70                                                          # loop 40: follows the roll axis (83) and the collar lugs (r 52)
    c = Pos(-40, (10 + y1) / 2, (38 + 160 + dz) / 2) * soft_shell(146, y1 - 10, 122 + dz, 42, 30, 2.0)   # loop 22: +8 mm back and up over the roll torque fork
    c -= Pos(-40, (10 + y1) / 2, 38) * Box(152, y1, 4)                        # open bottom (rear part)
    c -= Pos(14, (10 + y1) / 2, 38 + 12) * Box(40, y1, 28)                    # front part opens higher (roll pulley sweep)
    c -= Pos(-8, ROLL_Y, 0) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(_R_ROLL + 5, 30)      # clearance around the roll pulley
    return c - roll_sweep_envelope()


def roll_sweep_envelope(pad=3.0, step=5):
    """Loop 40 (rnd/swept.py): the space the roll output sweeps over roll −15…100° (yaw block + band, yaw cartridge +
    collar, as padded boxes rotated about the roll axis). Static covers are cut by it: the first swept check found the
    shoulder cap in the path of the yaw block and cartridge at roll 55–95° (true since loop 22, invisible to static checks)."""
    from build123d import Axis
    from ..twin import JR
    boxes = [(-130 - pad, 56 + pad, ROLL_Y - 50 - pad, ROLL_Y + 50 + pad, P2[0] - pad, P1[1] + pad),          # plates + band
             (-132 - pad, -30 + pad, ROLL_Y - 53 - pad, ROLL_Y + 53 + pad, ZF - 12 - pad, ZF + ZC + 10 + pad)]   # yaw cartridge, collar, fork
    ax = Axis((0, ROLL_Y, 0), (1, 0, 0))
    env = None
    for t in list(range(JR["roll"][0] - 5, JR["roll"][1] + 6, step)):
        for x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1 in boxes:
            b = Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * Box(x1 - x0, y1 - y0, z1 - z0)
            b = b.rotate(ax, t)
            env = b if env is None else env + b
    return env


def yaw_band() -> Part:
    """Rounded band around the yaw-block plates (roll body)."""
    from .soft import soft_box
    x0, x1 = -YAW_C - 50, 56                  # loop 40: +6 at +X for the ribbon from the roll spring
    zc, hz = (P2[0] + P1[1]) / 2, P1[1] - P2[0] + 3
    b = Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, ROLL_Y, zc) * (soft_box(x1 - x0, 100, hz, 30, 6) - soft_box(x1 - x0 - 4, 96, hz + 2, 28, 0.6))
    return b


def module():
    out = [("ferrule_m", Rot(-90, 0, 0) * ferrule(male=True), "orange"),
           ("roll_link", roll_link(), "white"), ("roll_shaft", roll_shaft(), "orange"), ("roll_pulley72", roll_pulley(), "orange"),
           ("roll_spring_hub", roll_spring_hub(), "dark"),
           ("yaw_block", clevis_and_yaw_block(), "white"), ("yaw_shaft", yaw_shaft(), "orange"), ("yaw_pulley40", yaw_pulley(), "orange"),
           ("yaw_spring_ring", yaw_spring_ring(), "dark")]
    out += [("shoulder_cap", shoulder_cap(), "white"), ("yaw_band", yaw_band(), "white")]
    out += [(f"roll_{n}", p, c) for n, p, c in _stage(ROLL_T, 18)]
    out += [(f"yaw_{n}", p, c) for n, p, c in _stage(YAW_T, 20)]
    out.append(("yaw_stop_pin", yaw_stop_pin(), "steel"))
    return out


def masses(density=1.27, split=False):
    """CAD roll-up (loop 40). Printed parts at 60–80 % fill; D13 standard parts by material (steel shaft, aluminium
    flange coupling and pulley, 6801 at 60 % of solid steel). split=True → (roll-body mass, yaw-stage mass) for params."""
    from .belt_stage import small_stage_v2
    st = dict((n, p) for n, p, _ in small_stage_v2(20))
    d13 = (st["shaft_12"].volume * 7.85e-3 + (st["flange_coupling"].volume + st["al_pulley"].volume) * 2.7e-3
           + (st["brg_A_6801"].volume + st["brg_B_6801"].volume) * 7.85e-3 * 0.6) / 1e3          # one stage, kg
    sock = (st["kx_hub_v2"].volume + collar(KX74).volume) * 0.7 / 1e6 * density
    pr = lambda v, f: v * f / 1e6 * density
    roll = {"roll link + spring ring/bar (printed)": pr(roll_link().volume, 0.6), "roll shaft + 72T pulley + spring hub": pr(roll_shaft().volume + roll_pulley().volume, 0.7) + pr(roll_spring_hub().volume, 0.8),
            "roll D13 stage + KX74 socket": d13 + sock, "2 × 61808 (ESTIMATE)": 0.04, "male ferrule (printed) + dowels + belt + screws + ribbon (ESTIMATE)": pr(67213, 0.7) + 0.05}
    yaw = {"clevis + yaw block plates + posts (printed)": pr(clevis_and_yaw_block().volume, 0.6),
           "yaw shaft + flange + 40T pulley + spring ring": pr(yaw_shaft().volume + yaw_pulley().volume, 0.7) + pr(yaw_spring_ring().volume, 0.8),
           "yaw D13 stage + KX74 socket": d13 + sock, "2 × 61808 + stop pin + belt + screws + ribbon (ESTIMATE)": 0.075}
    if split:
        return sum(roll.values()), sum(yaw.values())
    return {**{"roll: " + k: v for k, v in roll.items()}, **{"yaw: " + k: v for k, v in yaw.items()}}

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/shoulder.py ===
```python
"""Shoulder-pitch module v0 (torso side of the arm-detach plane). Left arm; outboard = +Y. Pitch axis = Y at the origin.

- Pitch cartridge M (GIM6010-8) behind the axis (x = -BELT_C), its output facing outboard, on a small-pulley hub.
- HTD 5M 20T → 60T belt (3:1, loop 10) in the belt plane; the 50T pulley sits on the pitch shaft (Ø40, 2 × 61808 in the bracket walls).
- Balancer plane, outboard of the bracket: a lever on the pitch shaft carries anchor pin B (30 mm); pulley A rides in a vertical slot
  45–65 mm above the axis; the cable runs back over A into a horizontal cage with a 2.7 N/mm compression spring (rnd/balancer.py).
- Outboard end of the shaft: the **KX-V ferrule** (3" sanitary profile, 3 dowels): the arm clamps on here with a V-band.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

from build123d import Align, Axis, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, fillet

from . import cartridge_m as cm
from .belt_stage import pulley, small_hub
from .kx import KX74, collar

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
WALL = 1.6
BELT_C = 70.0
# Layout along Y follows the small-pulley hub stack (hub face at Y_F; cartridge inboard of it):
Y_F = -78.0                # KX74 hub face of the pitch cartridge (loop 39: -58 → -78 for the 64 mm D13 v2 stack; can 1.3 mm clear of the lift carriage)
# loop 39: walls and belt plane derived from the D13 small stage v2 stack (stage z → y = Y_F − z)
from .belt_stage import V2 as _V2
WALL_T = 6.0
W_IN = Y_F - (_V2["jA"][1] + _V2["jA"][0]) / 2 - 0.5               # 6 mm wall, bearing A flush on the pulley side (−58.3 … −52.3)
```
