```python
W_OUT = Y_F - (_V2["jB"][1] + _V2["jB"][0]) / 2 + 0.5               # 6 mm wall, bearing B flush on the pulley side (−24.3 … −18.3)
Y_BELT = Y_F - _V2["belt_c"]                                        # belt plane
Y_BAL = -3.0               # balancer plane (outboard of the outer wall); loop 39: -6 → -3 (post ≥ 7.8 mm, 2.5 mm to the ferrule spigot)
Y_FER = 2.0                # ferrule spigot start; the detach plane (flat face) is at Y_FER + 18 = 20
FERRULE = dict(od=91.0, t=8.0, taper_deg=20.0, dowel_r=30.0)


def _rot_y(p):            # build along Z, then lay along +Y
    return Rot(-90, 0, 0) * p


def ferrule(male: bool = True) -> Part:
    """3"-style sanitary ferrule flange (printed). Profile ESTIMATE until checked against a real clamp."""
    f = FERRULE
    t_flat = 3.0
    p = Cylinder(f["od"] / 2, t_flat, align=MIN)
    # 20° taper on the back face (where the clamp wedges): approximated by a cone frustum
    from build123d import Cone
    p += Pos(0, 0, t_flat) * Cone(f["od"] / 2, f["od"] / 2 - (f["t"] - t_flat) * math.tan(math.radians(90 - f["taper_deg"])) * 0.35,
                                  f["t"] - t_flat, align=MIN)
    p += Pos(0, 0, f["t"]) * Cylinder(32, 10, align=MIN)                   # spigot toward the shaft / roll link
    for k in range(3):
        a = math.radians(90 + 120 * k)
        hole = Pos(f["dowel_r"] * math.cos(a), f["dowel_r"] * math.sin(a), 0) * Cylinder(3.0 - (0.05 if male else -0.05), 12, align=MIN)
        p -= hole
    return p - Cylinder(15, 40, align=MIN)


def pitch_shaft() -> Part:
    """Ø40 hollow shaft with the balancer disc (pin B) and the ferrule on the outboard end. Loop 40 (R14): the 60T pulley
    is a separate part (pitch_pulley) on a double-D, so the shaft goes in from outboard through W_OUT's 61808, the pulley
    and W_IN's 61808."""
    L = Y_FER - (W_IN - 4)
    from .belt_stage import dd_flats
    tube = Cylinder(20, L, align=MIN) - Cylinder(15, L, align=MIN)
    tube -= dd_flats(Y_BELT - 8.5 - (W_IN - 4), 17)                        # loop 40 (R14): double-D under the split 60T pulley
    s = Pos(0, W_IN - 4, 0) * _rot_y(tube)
    disc = Cylinder(25, 5, align=MIN) - Cylinder(15, 5, align=MIN)
    disc += Pos(0, -17, 0) * Box(14, 26, 5, align=MIN)                      # lever
    disc += Pos(0, -30, 0) * Cylinder(3, 9, align=MIN)                      # pin B, 30 mm from the axis (points down when the arm hangs)
    s += Pos(0, Y_BAL - 2.5, 0) * _rot_y(disc)
    s += Pos(0, Y_FER + 18, 0) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * ferrule(male=False)       # flat face outboard at the detach plane
    return s


def pitch_pulley() -> Part:
    from .belt_stage import split_pulley
    return Pos(0, Y_BELT - 8, 0) * _rot_y(split_pulley(60))


def bracket() -> Part:
    """Printed bracket: two walls with bearing bosses + a back web + the balancer slot + spring cage."""
    wall_t = WALL_T
    xs, zs = 190.0, 150.0
    x0 = -BELT_C / 2 - 10
    walls = None
    for y in (W_IN, W_OUT):
        w = Pos(x0, y, 10) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * fillet(Box(xs, zs, wall_t).edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), 20)
        w -= Pos(0, y, 0) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(26, wall_t + 1)                  # 61808 seat (OD 52)
        w -= Pos(-BELT_C, y, 0) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(19.1, wall_t + 1)          # eccentric-bushing seat (Ø38) around the small hub's 61804
        # lightening (loop 8 diet): keep ≥ 10 mm of material around both bearing seats and along the back web
        for cx, cz, lx, lz in ((-75, 62, 96, 22), (-62, -52, 110, 18), (-128, 0, 14, 60)):
            w -= Pos(cx, y, cz) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * fillet(Box(lx, lz, wall_t + 1).edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), 5)
        walls = w if walls is None else walls + w
    web = Pos(x0 - xs / 2 + 4, (W_IN + W_OUT) / 2, 10) * Box(8, W_OUT - W_IN, zs)
    p = walls + web
    # balancer slot: pulley A axle rides 35–65 mm above the axis in the inboard wall
    post_y0, post_y1 = W_OUT + WALL_T / 2, Y_BAL - 3.5                                     # post beside pulley A (axle cantilevered)
    post = Pos(0, (post_y0 + post_y1) / 2, 58) * Box(16, post_y1 - post_y0, 40)
    post -= Pos(0, (post_y0 + post_y1) / 2, 55) * Box(4.2, 40, 24)                      # vertical slot: a = 45–65 mm
    p += post
    # spring cage: horizontal tube running back from pulley A (cable leaves A's top toward -X), above the small hub
    cage = Pos(-15, Y_BAL, 58) * Rot(0, -90, 0) * (Cylinder(14, 135, align=MIN) - Cylinder(14 - WALL, 135, align=MIN))
    return p + cage + torque_fork()


def torque_fork() -> Part:
    """Loop 21: torque reaction for the pitch cartridge. Its Ø6 pin sits 30 mm toward the pitch axis (+X) from the
    cartridge axis, behind the can rear (−Y). A deep side plate (6 mm, two 18.5 mm flanges at z ±26.5…45 around the collar clearance) runs inboard from the inner wall beside
    the can and carries a 7 mm fork plate with an open radial slot (open toward the cartridge axis). Load ±Z, 367 N at
    the GIM peak: plate bending ≈ 4.6 MPa (hand calc). The collar is still turned from the open side (−X)."""
    tp = cm.TORQUE_PIN
    zc = KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]
    xa, xp = -BELT_C, -BELT_C + tp["r"]                               # cartridge axis, pin
    y_rear = Y_F - zc                                                  # can rear face
    y_wall = W_IN - WALL_T / 2                                         # inner face of the inner wall
    y_far = y_rear - 9
    side = Pos(-24, (y_wall + y_far) / 2, 0) * Box(6, y_wall - y_far, 90)
    side -= Pos(xa, 0, 0) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(KX74.r_collar_out + 1.3, 400)   # clear the KX collar (r 49.2) and the can: two 18.5 mm flanges remain (≈ 4.6 MPa)
    fork = Pos((xp - 9 + -21) / 2, y_rear - 5.5, 0) * Box(-21 - (xp - 9), 7, 48)
    fork -= Pos(xp - 4, y_rear - 5.5, 0) * Box(18, 8, tp["d"] + 0.4)                                 # open radial slot
    return side + fork


def pulley_a() -> Part:
    return Pos(0, Y_BAL, 50) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * (Cylinder(8, 6) - Cylinder(2, 6))


def module(with_motor: bool = True):
    """(name, part, colour) list in the module frame. Stage frame (as in arm.assembly): hub face at z=0, cartridge +Z;
    mapped so stage +Z → world -Y, hub face at Y_F, axis at x = -BELT_C."""
    st = Pos(-BELT_C, Y_F, 0) * Rot(90, 0, 0)
    zc = KX74.gap + KX74.face_t + cm.NECK + cm.FLOOR + cm.MOTOR["h"]
    out = [("bracket", bracket(), "white"), ("shaft", pitch_shaft(), "orange"), ("pitch_pulley60", pitch_pulley(), "orange"), ("pulleyA", pulley_a(), "steel"),
           ("collar", st * collar(KX74), "orange")]
    from .belt_stage import small_stage_v2
    out += [(n, st * pp, c) for n, pp, c in small_stage_v2(20)]                      # loop 39: D13 stage v2
    if with_motor:
        out += [("can", st * Pos(0, 0, zc) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.can(), "white"),
                ("motor", st * Pos(0, 0, zc) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * cm.motor_dummy(), "dark")]
    return out


def masses(density=1.27):
    m = {"bracket (walls 8 mm, web, cage)": bracket().volume / 1e6 * density * 0.6,
         "pitch shaft + split 60T pulley + balancer disc + ferrule": (pitch_shaft().volume + pitch_pulley().volume) / 1e6 * density * 0.7,
         "small-pulley hub + KX74 socket (collar, retainer)": (small_hub().volume + collar(KX74).volume) / 1e6 * density * 0.7,
         "bearings 2×61808 + 2×61804 (ESTIMATE)": 0.056,
         "spring 2 N/mm + cable + pulley A + axle (ESTIMATE)": 0.09,
         "V-band clamp, stainless 3\" (ESTIMATE)": 0.11}
    return m

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/soft.py ===
```python
"""Soft forms for the industrial-design pass: boxes and shells with every edge rounded (pill-like, robot-friendly)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from build123d import Axis, Box, fillet


def soft_box(x, y, z, rv, re=None):
    """Box with vertical edges rounded by rv and top/bottom edges by re (≤ rv)."""
    rv = min(rv, x / 2 - 0.4, y / 2 - 0.4)
    re = min(re if re is not None else rv * 0.6, z / 2 - 0.4, rv - 0.2)
    b = Box(x, y, z)
    if rv > 0.5:
        b = fillet(b.edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), rv)
    if re > 0.5:
        try:
            b = fillet(b.edges().group_by(Axis.Z)[0] + b.edges().group_by(Axis.Z)[-1], re)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return b


def soft_shell(x, y, z, rv, re=None, t=2.0):
    re = re if re is not None else rv * 0.6
    return soft_box(x, y, z, rv, re) - soft_box(x - 2 * t, y - 2 * t, z - 2 * t, max(rv - t, 1), max(re - t, 0.6))

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/torque.py ===
```python
"""Torque-reaction pads for small cartridges (loop 25): find a can's torque pin from its seat geometry and build a
slotted pad around the pin's tip, fused to the part the cartridge is mounted on.

The pad is a block (radial × tangential × axial) centred on the pin's protruding length. A slot (pin Ø + 0.4) runs
radially through it, open toward the cartridge axis so the cartridge keeps its radial float. If the pad doesn't touch the
anchor part, a Ø10 bridge joins it to the anchor's nearest point.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
from build123d import Box, Cylinder, Part, Plane, Pos


def _seat(part, r_seat, tol=0.06):
    from OCP.BRepAdaptor import BRepAdaptor_Surface
    from OCP.GeomAbs import GeomAbs_Cylinder
    for f in part.faces():
        s = BRepAdaptor_Surface(f.wrapped)
        if s.GetType() == GeomAbs_Cylinder and abs(s.Cylinder().Radius() - r_seat) < tol:
            ax = s.Cylinder().Axis()
            d = np.array([ax.Direction().X(), ax.Direction().Y(), ax.Direction().Z()])
            loc = np.array([ax.Location().X(), ax.Location().Y(), ax.Location().Z()])
            c = np.array([f.center().X, f.center().Y, f.center().Z])
            return loc + np.dot(c - loc, d) * d, d
    raise ValueError("no torque-pin seat found")


def pin_frame(can, r_seat):
    """(seat centre on the pin axis, outward pin direction, radial unit vector from the cartridge axis)."""
    c, ax = _seat(can, r_seat)
    cen = np.array([can.center().X, can.center().Y, can.center().Z])
    out = ax if np.dot(c - cen, ax) > 0 else -ax
    rad = (c - cen) - np.dot(c - cen, out) * out
    return c, out, rad / np.linalg.norm(rad)


def with_pin_slot(can, anchor, r_seat, r_pin, floor=2.5, z0=3.0, t=6.0, width=14.0, out_len=6.0, in_len=10.0) -> Part:
    """`anchor` + a slotted pad around the pin (z0 … z0 + t beyond the seat centre along the pin) + a Ø10 bridge if the pad
    doesn't touch the anchor; the slot is cut through pad *and* anchor (from the can's rear face outward), open toward the
    cartridge axis, and the can itself is kept clear."""
    c, out, rad = pin_frame(can, r_seat)
    pl = Plane(origin=tuple(c + out * z0), x_dir=tuple(rad), z_dir=tuple(out))
    pad = pl.location * (Pos((out_len - in_len) / 2, 0, 0) * Box(out_len + in_len, width, t, align=(_c(), _c(), _min())))
    d, p_pad, p_anc = pad.distance_to_with_closest_points(anchor)
    if d > 0.05:
        a, b = np.array([p_pad.X, p_pad.Y, p_pad.Z]), np.array([p_anc.X, p_anc.Y, p_anc.Z])
        v = (b - a) / np.linalg.norm(b - a)
        pad = pad + Plane(origin=tuple(a - v * 1.5), z_dir=tuple(v)).location * Cylinder(5, d + 3.0, align=(_c(), _c(), _min()))
    s0 = floor / 2 + 0.2                                             # from just behind the can's rear face
    slot = Plane(origin=tuple(c + out * s0), x_dir=tuple(rad), z_dir=tuple(out)).location * (
        Pos((r_pin + 1.5 - in_len - 2) / 2, 0, 0) * Box(r_pin + 1.5 + in_len + 2, 2 * r_pin + 0.4, z0 + t + 0.5 - s0, align=(_c(), _c(), _min())))
    return anchor + pad - slot - can


def _c():
    from build123d import Align
    return Align.CENTER


def _min():
    from build123d import Align
    return Align.MIN

```

=== FILE: rnd/parts/upper.py ===
```python
"""Mast, lift, torso and head v0 (robot frame: x forward, z up; mast axis at x = MAST_X).

- Mast: 60×60×2 Al square tube from the base coupling to the head bracket (rnd/mast.py).
- Lift: GIM cartridge at the mast foot drives a 20T HTD 5M pulley; the belt loops over an idler in the top bracket and
  clamps to the carriage. Printed ratchet strip on the mast's back face; spring-applied pawl + 12 V solenoid on the
  carriage (rnd/lift.py).
- Carriage: printed sleeve with 8 × 625 bearings on the tube faces; the torso shell and both pitch modules bolt to it.
- Torso: two-piece shell with a chest light; electronics tray (Pi 5, CAN, DC-DC, fuses) on the back; e-stop on top.
- Head (fixed on the mast top, D8): top bracket (idler + carry handle) → pan cartridge S (vertical) → yoke →
  tilt cartridge S → head shell with a dark visor, eye lights and the RGB-D camera.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

from build123d import Align, Axis, Box, Cylinder, Part, Pos, Rot, fillet

from ..params import BASELINE
from . import cartridge_m as cm, cartridge_s as cs
from .belt_stage import pulley
from .soft import soft_box, soft_shell

MIN = (Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)
D = BASELINE
MAST_X = -60.0
W = D.upper.mast_w * 1000
Z_BASE = (D.base.clearance + D.base.height) * 1000
Z_TOP = Z_BASE + D.upper.spine_len * 1000
HEAD_Z = Z_TOP + 10.0
WALL = 2.0


def _rbox(x, y, z, r):
    r = min(r, x / 2 - 0.3, y / 2 - 0.3)
    return fillet(Box(x, y, z).edges().filter_by(Axis.Z), r) if r > 0.5 else Box(x, y, z)


def _shell(x, y, z, r, t=WALL):
    return _rbox(x, y, z, r) - _rbox(x - 2 * t, y - 2 * t, z - 2 * t, max(r - t, 1))


def mast() -> Part:
    L = Z_TOP - Z_BASE
    return Pos(MAST_X, 0, Z_BASE) * (Box(W, W, L, align=MIN) - Box(W - 4, W - 4, L, align=MIN))


def ratchet_strip() -> Part:
    """Printed in 4 segments (each ≤ 256 mm) bonded to the mast's back face."""
    z0, z1 = Z_BASE + 170, Z_TOP - 60
    seg = (z1 - z0) / 4
    p = None
    for i in range(4):
        s = Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 3, 23, z0 + i * seg) * Box(6, 14, seg - 1, align=MIN)
        for k in range(int(seg // 20)):                                  # visual teeth every 20 mm (real pitch 5 mm)
            s -= Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 6, 23, z0 + i * seg + 10 + 20 * k) * Rot(0, 45, 0) * Box(4, 16, 4)
        p = s if p is None else p + s
    return p


def lift_drive():
    z = Z_BASE + 40 + 70                                              # mount sits on top of the base coupling block
    T = Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 50, -20, z) * Rot(90, 0, 0)          # cartridge axis along -Y, behind the mast
    zc = cm.stack_height()
    return [("lift_can", T * Pos(0, 0, 0) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * Pos(0, 0, -zc) * cm.can(), "white"),
            ("lift_motor", T * Pos(0, 0, 0) * Rot(180, 0, 0) * Pos(0, 0, -zc) * cm.motor_dummy(), "dark"),
            ("lift_pulley", Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 50, -10, z) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * pulley(20, 16), "orange"),
            ("lift_belt", Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 50 + 16, -2, z) * Box(3.6, 15, Z_TOP - z - 10, align=MIN), "dark"),
            ("lift_mount", lift_mount(z, zc), "white")]


def lift_mount(z, zc):
    """Mount block at the mast foot + the lift cartridge's torque fork (loop 20). The Ø6 torque pin sits 30 mm from the
    cartridge axis toward the mast (+X) and sticks out of the can rear (−Y). A 7 mm fork plate on an arm from the block's
    −Y face catches it. The slot is open toward the cartridge axis (radial float); torque load is vertical (±Z)."""
    xa = MAST_X - W / 2 - 50                                           # cartridge axis x
    m = Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 30, -20, Z_BASE + 40) * Box(60, 90, 120, align=MIN)
    m -= Pos(xa, -20, z) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(45, 200)
    tp = cm.TORQUE_PIN
    xp, y_out = xa + tp["r"], -20 - zc                                  # pin x; can rear face (y)
    xr = MAST_X - W / 2                                                # mast face
    m += Pos((xp + 14.3 + xr) / 2, (-65 + y_out - 9) / 2, z) * Box(xr - xp - 14.3, -65 - (y_out - 9), 48)      # arm beside the can (can r 43.3)
    m += Pos((xp - 7 + xr) / 2, y_out - 5.5, z) * Box(xr - xp + 7, 7, 48)                                       # fork plate
    m -= Pos(xp + 1.5, y_out - 5.5, z) * Box(17, 8, tp["d"] + 0.4)                                              # open radial slot
    m -= Pos(xa + 16, -2, z) * Box(3.6 + 6, 15 + 6, 400, align=MIN)        # R15: belt ≥ 3 mm from static parts                                            # loop 31: belt slot (the belt ran through the block)
    return m


def lift_cover() -> Part:
    """Looks pass v2: rounded cover over the lift cartridge, pulley and mount at the mast foot (slot for the belt)."""
    x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1 = -194.0, -91.0, -91.0, 36.0, Z_BASE + 41, Z_BASE + 172      # y0 −88 → −91: clears the torque-pin tip (loop 20)
    c = Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * soft_shell(x1 - x0, y1 - y0, z1 - z0, 30, 22, 2.0)
    c -= Pos((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, z0) * Box(x1 - x0 + 2, y1 - y0 + 2, 6)      # open bottom
    c -= Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 34, -2, z1) * Box(26, 22, 10)                           # belt slot
    c -= Pos(x1, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2) * Box(36, y1 - y0 + 4, z1 - z0 + 4)       # open against the mast / mount face
    zl = Z_BASE + 40 + 70
    c -= lift_mount(zl, cm.stack_height())                                            # loop 31: no overlap with the mount it covers
    return c


def carriage(lift_mm: float) -> Part:
    h = 160.0
    c = Pos(MAST_X, 0, lift_mm - h / 2) * (Box(W + 26, W + 26, h, align=MIN) - Box(W + 2, W + 2, h, align=MIN))
    for z in (lift_mm - h / 2 + 18, lift_mm + h / 2 - 18):           # 625 bearing bosses, 2 per face
        for dx, dy in ((W / 2 + 7, 0), (-W / 2 - 7, 0), (0, W / 2 + 7), (0, -W / 2 - 7)):
            c += Pos(MAST_X + dx, dy, z) * Box(14 if dx else 30, 30 if dx else 14, 24)
    c -= Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 6, 23, lift_mm) * Box(14, 18, 400)                 # slot over the ratchet strip
    # pawl + solenoid on the back, beside the slot
    c += Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 20, 23, lift_mm - 30) * Box(14, 22, 40)
    return c


def torso(lift_mm: float):
    # Looks pass (owner request): a rounded body that encloses both shoulder pitch modules; the arms emerge through
    # round ports at the shoulders. Printed as left/right halves (each 185 mm wide).
    x0 = 15.0
    shell = Pos(x0, 0, lift_mm) * soft_shell(230, 390, 210, 70, 40, 2.0)            # clears the pitch-bracket corners
    shell -= Pos(x0, 0, lift_mm) * Box(400, 1.0, 400)                                 # split line
    shell -= Pos(MAST_X, 0, lift_mm) * Box(W + 30, W + 30, 400)                       # mast + carriage passage
    for s in (1, -1):                                                                  # shoulder ports (pitch shaft + V-band)
        shell -= Pos(D.arm.shoulder_x * 1000, s * 195, lift_mm) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(50, 40)
    # electronics in front of the pitch cartridges (they reach into the torso at x −53 … 33)
    tray = Pos(75, 0, lift_mm - 40) * Box(60, 150, 6)
    pi = Pos(75, -20, lift_mm - 25.5) * Box(56, 85, 22)
    dcdc = Pos(75, 50, lift_mm - 23.5) * Box(50, 46, 26)
    ai_bay = Pos(75, 0, lift_mm + 25) * (Box(105, 95, 40) - Box(101, 91, 40))          # D10: reserved Jetson-class AI computer bay
    estop = Pos(-30, 80, lift_mm + 105) * Cylinder(15, 14, align=MIN)
    light = Pos(x0 + 116.5, 0, lift_mm - 30) * soft_box(3, 160, 8, 1.2, 0.6)
    return [("torso_shell", shell, "white"), ("ai_bay", ai_bay, "dark"), ("e_tray", tray, "dark"), ("e_pi5", pi, "dark"), ("e_dcdc", dcdc, "dark"),
            ("estop", estop, "orange"), ("chest_light", light, "orange")]


def head(pan_deg: float = 0.0, tilt_deg: float = 0.0):
    top = Pos(MAST_X, 0, Z_TOP) * Box(W + 16, W + 16, 24, align=MIN)                  # top bracket on the mast
    top -= Pos(MAST_X, 0, Z_TOP) * Box(W + 0.4, W + 0.4, 10, align=MIN)
    handle = Pos(MAST_X - 50, 0, Z_TOP + 24) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * (Cylinder(34, 24) - Cylinder(24, 25) - Pos(0, -40, 0) * Box(80, 80, 30))
    idler = Pos(MAST_X - W / 2 - 34, 0, Z_TOP + 8) * Rot(90, 0, 0) * Cylinder(14, 18)
    # pan cartridge S, shaft up, on the top bracket; yoke + head ride on it
    pan = Pos(MAST_X, 0, Z_TOP + 24) * Rot(0, 0, 90) * cs.sleeve()
    yz = Z_TOP + 24 + cs.stack_height()
    P = Pos(MAST_X, 0, yz) * Rot(0, 0, pan_deg)
    yoke = Box(60, 150, 8, align=MIN) + Pos(0, 71, 0) * Box(40, 8, 60, align=MIN) + Pos(0, -71, 0) * Box(40, 8, 60, align=MIN)
    tilt = Pos(0, 75, 40) * Rot(-90, 0, 0) * cs.sleeve()
    # loop 25: slotted torque pads for the pan and tilt cartridges' Ø4 pins (rnd/parts/torque.py)
    from .torque import with_pin_slot
    top = with_pin_slot(pan, top, 1.95, 2.0)
    yoke = with_pin_slot(tilt, yoke, 1.95, 2.0)
    T = Pos(0, 0, 40) * Rot(0, tilt_deg, 0)
    # D11 (loop 28): face screen. The shell grows 16 mm upward (120 → 136) so the 4.3" display board sits behind a flat
    # dark bezel with the depth camera below it.
    shell = Pos(70, 0, 13) * soft_shell(160, 244, 136, 55, 38, 2.4)                 # pill-shaped head
    shell -= Pos(150, 0, 16) * Box(20, 200, 86)                                      # bezel opening
    shell -= Pos(135, 0, -36) * Box(40, 96, 29)                                       # depth-camera window (Gemini 2)
    shell -= Pos(-20, 0, -20) * Box(110, 180, 90)                                    # neck opening at the back/bottom (yoke + pan servo)
    # bezel (dark PETG) with the screen window; Waveshare ESP32-S3-Touch-LCD-4.3B (112.4 × 75.1 mm board, 4.3" 800×480
    # capacitive touch, CAN transceiver, 7–36 V input: runs straight off the 24 V bus) behind it
    # loop 28: clearance for the tilt and pan cartridges over the whole tilt range (±30°) and any pan angle. The shell
    # used to clip the tilt sleeve (0.4 cm³) and, at +30° tilt, the pan sleeve.
    tb = tilt.bounding_box()
    env_t = Pos(tb.center().X, tb.center().Y, tb.center().Z) * Box(tb.size.X + 4, tb.size.Y + 4, tb.size.Z + 4)
    pb = (Pos(-MAST_X, 0, -yz) * pan).bounding_box()                                 # pan sleeve in the yoke (P) frame
    r_pan = max(math.hypot(x, y) for x in (pb.min.X, pb.max.X) for y in (pb.min.Y, pb.max.Y)) + 2
    env_p = Pos(0, 0, pb.min.Z - 2) * Cylinder(r_pan, pb.size.Z + 4, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))
    for td in range(-30, 31, 10):
        inv = (Pos(0, 0, 40) * Rot(0, td, 0)).inverse()
        shell -= inv * env_t
        shell -= inv * env_p
    visor = Pos(146, 0, 16) * soft_box(6, 196, 86, 2.8, 1.5) - Pos(146, 0, 16) * Box(10, 97, 56)
    screen = Pos(138, 0, 16) * Box(9, 112.4, 75.1)
    eyes = [Pos(142.8, s * 22, 18) * Rot(0, 90, 0) * Cylinder(9, 0.6) for s in (1, -1)]       # drawn on the screen (render only)
    cam = Pos(128, 0, -36) * Box(30, 90, 25)                                         # Gemini 2, below the bezel
    out = [("head_bracket", top, "white"), ("handle", handle, "dark"), ("belt_idler", idler, "steel"),
           ("pan_sleeve", pan, "white"), ("yoke", P * yoke, "dark"), ("tilt_sleeve", P * tilt, "white"),
           ("head_shell", P * T * shell, "white"), ("visor", P * T * visor, "dark"),
           ("head_screen", P * T * screen, "black"),
           ("eye_l", P * T * eyes[0], "orange"), ("eye_r", P * T * eyes[1], "orange"), ("head_cam", P * T * cam, "dark")]
    return out


def module(lift_m: float = 0.75):
    L = lift_m * 1000
    return [("mast", mast(), "black"), ("ratchet", ratchet_strip(), "dark"), *lift_drive(), ("lift_cover", lift_cover(), "white"),
            ("carriage", carriage(L), "white"), *torso(L), *head()]


def masses(density=1.27):
    L = 750.0
    tor = {n: p for n, p, c in torso(L)}
    hd = {n: p for n, p, c in head()}
    return {
        "carriage (printed) + 8×625 bearings + pawl/solenoid": carriage(L).volume / 1e6 * density * 0.6 + 8 * 0.012 + 0.06,
        "torso shell + tray (printed)": (tor["torso_shell"].volume + tor["e_tray"].volume) / 1e6 * density,
        "ratchet strip (printed)": ratchet_strip().volume / 1e6 * density,
        "lift mount (printed) + belt 2.4 m + idler": [p for n, p, c in lift_drive() if n == "lift_mount"][0].volume / 1e6 * density * 0.5 + 0.10,
        "head: bracket, handle, yoke, shell, visor (printed)": sum(hd[k].volume for k in ("head_bracket", "handle", "yoke", "head_shell", "visor")) / 1e6 * density * 0.8,
        "head: 2 × STS3215 + sleeves + RGB-D camera (99 g) + eye LEDs": 2 * 0.055 + 2 * 0.02 + 0.10 + 0.02,
    }

```

=== FILE: rnd/spring.py ===
```python
"""Shoulder gravity-balance study: how much of the arm's weight can a pitch-only spring really cancel?

arm_sizing.py assumed a spring cancels a fixed 85 % of the arm's self-weight moment in every pose. A real balancer
gives τ_s = K·sin(pitch) (zero-free-length spring + cable, the Anglepoise / Herder gravity-balancing principle).
When the elbow bends or the arm rolls sideways, the arm's centre of mass moves and the balance drifts.

This samples the joint space (MuJoCo twin, static gravity torques), fits K, and reports the motor torque left over.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np

from .arm_sizing import BELT_EFF
from .params import BASELINE, Design, act
from .twin import JR, build_xml, mujoco

N = 6000
SEED = 4


def _model(d):
    m = mujoco.MjModel.from_xml_string(build_xml(d))
    return m, mujoco.MjData(m)


def gravity_torques(d: Design, n=N, seed=SEED, pitch_range=None):
    """Static pitch-joint torque (N·m, at the joint) for random right-arm poses. Returns (pitch_rad, torque)."""
    m, dat = _model(d)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    jid = {j: mujoco.mj_name2id(m, mujoco.mjtObj.mjOBJ_JOINT, f"{j}_r") for j in ("pitch", "roll", "yaw", "elbow", "wpitch", "wroll")}
    lo, hi = pitch_range or JR["pitch"]
    ranges = {"pitch": (lo, hi), "roll": JR["roll"], "yaw": JR["yaw"], "elbow": JR["elbow"],
              "wpitch": (-d.arm.wrist_pitch_range_deg, d.arm.wrist_pitch_range_deg), "wroll": (0, 0)}
    p_out, t_out = np.empty(n), np.empty(n)
    for i in range(n):
        dat.qpos[:] = 0
        dat.qvel[:] = 0
        for j, (a, b) in ranges.items():
            dat.qpos[m.jnt_qposadr[jid[j]]] = math.radians(rng.uniform(a, b))
        mujoco.mj_forward(m, dat)
        p_out[i] = dat.qpos[m.jnt_qposadr[jid["pitch"]]]
        t_out[i] = dat.qfrc_bias[m.jnt_dofadr[jid["pitch"]]]
    return p_out, t_out


JOINTS = {"pitch": "shoulder_pitch", "roll": "shoulder_roll", "yaw": "upper_yaw", "elbow": "elbow", "wpitch": "wrist_pitch"}


def workspace_torques(d: Design = BASELINE, n=N, seed=SEED):
    """|static torque| per joint over random poses (payload included). The pitch joint includes the balancer."""
    m, dat = _model(d)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    jid = {j: mujoco.mj_name2id(m, mujoco.mjtObj.mjOBJ_JOINT, f"{j}_r") for j in JOINTS}
    ranges = {"pitch": JR["pitch"], "roll": JR["roll"], "yaw": JR["yaw"], "elbow": JR["elbow"],
              "wpitch": (-d.arm.wrist_pitch_range_deg, d.arm.wrist_pitch_range_deg)}
    T = {j: np.empty(n) for j in JOINTS}
    for i in range(n):
        dat.qpos[:] = 0
        dat.qvel[:] = 0
        for j, (a, b) in ranges.items():
            dat.qpos[m.jnt_qposadr[jid[j]]] = math.radians(rng.uniform(a, b))
        mujoco.mj_forward(m, dat)
        for j in JOINTS:
            t = dat.qfrc_bias[m.jnt_dofadr[jid[j]]]
            if j == "pitch":
                t -= d.arm.spring_k_nm * math.sin(dat.qpos[m.jnt_qposadr[jid[j]]])
            T[j][i] = abs(t)
    return T


def workspace_margins(d: Design = BASELINE):
    from .arm_sizing import capacity
    T = workspace_torques(d)
    out = {}
    for j, full in JOINTS.items():
        name, ratio, rated, peak, _ = capacity(d, full)
        p95, mx = float(np.quantile(T[j], .95)), float(T[j].max())
        out[full] = dict(act=name, ratio=ratio, p95=p95, max=mx, rated=rated, peak=peak, m_rated=rated / p95, m_peak=peak / mx)
    return out


def fit_balancer(p, t):
    """Least-squares K for τ_s = K·sin(p) (spring cancels the arm's own weight)."""
    s = np.sin(p)
    return float(np.dot(s, t) / np.dot(s, s))


def study(d: Design = BASELINE):
    d0 = d.copy(**{"arm.payload": 1e-4})
    p0, t0 = gravity_torques(d0)
    K_fit = fit_balancer(p0, t0)
    K = d.arm.spring_k_nm
    p1, t1 = gravity_torques(d)                       # same poses (same seed) with the payload
    res0, res1 = t0 - K * np.sin(p0), t1 - K * np.sin(p1)
    name, ratio = d.arm.joints["shoulder_pitch"]
    a = act(name)
    cap_rated, cap_peak = a["rated"] * ratio * BELT_EFF, a["peak"] * ratio * BELT_EFF
    q = lambda x, k: float(np.quantile(np.abs(x), k))
    return dict(K=K, K_fit=K_fit, cap_rated=cap_rated, cap_peak=cap_peak,
                no_spring=dict(p95=q(t1, .95), max=q(t1, 1.0)),
                arm_only=dict(p95=q(res0, .95), max=q(res0, 1.0), share=1 - q(res0, .95) / q(t0, .95)),
                with_payload=dict(p95=q(res1, .95), max=q(res1, 1.0)))


def report(d: Design = BASELINE) -> str:
    s = study(d)
    L = ["# Shoulder spring balance (auto-generated by `python -m rnd.run_all`)", "",
         f"{N} random right-arm poses across the full joint ranges (MuJoCo twin, static). A pitch-only balancer τ = K·sin(pitch) "
         "(zero-free-length spring + cable: Anglepoise / Herder gravity balancing) is fitted to the arm's own weight.", "",
         f"**Design K = {s['K']:.2f} N·m** (spring torque at the horizontal arm; `params.arm.spring_k_nm`). "
         f"A least-squares fit to the bare arm gives {s['K_fit']:.2f} N·m; a K sweep showed 4.0 minimises the 95th-percentile motor torque "
         "for a 0.5 kg typical payload (0.5 kg: 5.2 N·m; 1 kg: 7.3 N·m). The spring preload is screw-adjustable (Anglepoise precedent), so K can be re-tuned per task.", "",
         "| Motor torque needed at the pitch joint | 95th percentile | Worst pose |", "|---|---|---|",
         f"| No spring, 1 kg payload | {s['no_spring']['p95']:.1f} N·m | {s['no_spring']['max']:.1f} N·m |",
         f"| Spring, arm only | {s['arm_only']['p95']:.1f} N·m | {s['arm_only']['max']:.1f} N·m |",
         f"| **Spring, 1 kg payload** | **{s['with_payload']['p95']:.1f} N·m** | **{s['with_payload']['max']:.1f} N·m** |",
         f"| Joint capacity (GIM6010-8 × {BASELINE.arm.joints['shoulder_pitch'][1]} belt), rated / peak | {s['cap_rated']:.1f} | {s['cap_peak']:.1f} |", "",
         f"The spring cancels **{s['arm_only']['share']:.0%}** of the arm's self-weight torque at the 95th percentile (arm_sizing assumed 85 % everywhere).",
         f"Margin with payload: rated / 95th pct **{s['cap_rated'] / s['with_payload']['p95']:.2f}×**, peak / worst pose **{s['cap_peak'] / s['with_payload']['max']:.2f}×**.", "",
         "## Every arm joint over the whole workspace (1 kg payload, static, balancer on)", "",
         "The planar check in [arm sizing](arm_sizing.md) only looks at a straight horizontal arm. That pose never loads the upper-arm yaw, "
         "which holds the forearm + payload whenever the elbow is bent and the upper arm is not vertical (on a Franka arm that joint is as strong as the shoulder).", "",
         "| Joint | Actuator × ratio | Need: 95th pct / worst (N·m) | Rated / peak | Margin rated/p95 | Margin peak/worst |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for j, r in workspace_margins(d).items():
        f = "✅" if r["m_rated"] >= 1.5 and r["m_peak"] >= 1.5 else ("⚠️" if r["m_rated"] >= 1.0 and r["m_peak"] >= 1.0 else "❌")
        L.append(f"| {j} | {r['act']} × {r['ratio']:g} | {r['p95']:.1f} / {r['max']:.1f} | {r['rated']:.1f} / {r['peak']:.1f} | {r['m_rated']:.2f} | {r['m_peak']:.2f} {f} |")
    L += ["", "**Loop 5 change:** upper-arm yaw DM-J4310 direct (rated/p95 0.65×, ❌) → **GIM6010-8 × 2 belt**. "
          "It's cheaper (~$70 per arm), backdrivable, the same part as the other big joints, and adds +0.29 kg per arm. "
          "Considered: DM-J4340P direct (1.96×, +70 g, +$29, but 40:1 gearing is barely backdrivable)."]
    return "\n".join(L)


def score(d: Design = BASELINE):
    w = workspace_margins(d)
    return min(r["m_rated"] for r in w.values()), min(r["m_peak"] for r in w.values())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(report())

```

=== FILE: rnd/stiffness.py ===
```python
"""Hand-deflection compliance chain (queue item 3 + review A8, loop 40).  python -m rnd.stiffness

The scorecard's hand sag (rnd/bones.py) only bends the carbon tubes. The joint encoders sit on the joint outputs, so
they see and correct belt stretch and KX twist, but not anything that bends or tilts **after** a joint output. This
chain adds those terms for the worst static case: 1 kg at the grasp point, arm straight out horizontally.

1. **Printed structure (FEA).** The four parts that carry the whole arm moment are re-solved with quadratic (P2)
   tetrahedra, which the review asked for (P1 tets are too stiff in bending), and with P1 on the same mesh for the
   ratio. Each part's loaded bearing seats are fitted with a rigid motion (translation t, rotation ω); the hand moves
   by t + ω × r (r = seat → hand). FEA loads are the ×1.5 design cases; the chain uses them ÷ 1.5.
2. **Bearing play.** A 61808 pair with C0 radial clearance (≈ 10 µm each, ESTIMATE) tilts by 2·c/span. Shimmed
   (light axial preload) pairs remove most of it: the chain uses 30 % of the free play for shimmed pairs.
3. **Carbon tubes** (rnd/bones.py) as before.

The total is the static error a calibrated gravity model can largely remove (it is repeatable); the wrist camera closes
the loop for grasping in any case. Target R2b: uncompensated ≤ 6 mm, after the gravity model (assumed to remove 80 %) ≤ 2 mm.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

DOCS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "docs" / "rnd"
H = 4.5                     # mesh size for the P1/P2 pair (P2 at h 3 would be ≈ 1.5 M dof)
PLAY_MM, SHIM = 0.010, 0.3  # radial clearance per bearing (ESTIMATE), fraction left after shimming
COMP = 0.8                  # fraction removed by a calibrated gravity-deflection model (ESTIMATE until M1 measures it)


def run():
    from . import fea
    from .params import BASELINE as d
    a = d.arm
    reach = a.l_upper + a.l_fore + a.l_hand                       # pitch axis → grasp, arm straight out (m)
    want = {"Shoulder pitch bracket": reach * 1000, "Roll link": reach * 1000, "Clevis + yaw block": reach * 1000,
            "Upper bone / elbow housing": (a.l_fore + a.l_hand) * 1000}
    rows = []
    for name, part, h, fixed, loads in fea.cases():
        key = next((k for k in want if name.startswith(k)), None)
        if key is None:
            continue
        mesh = fea.mesh_part(part, H)
        r1 = fea.solve2(mesh, fixed, loads, order=1)
        r2 = fea.solve2(mesh, fixed, loads, order=2)
        L = want[key]
        # rotation of the loaded seats (mean over regions), ÷1.5 to the static case; hand moves |ω|·L + |t|
        w2 = np.mean([np.linalg.norm(g["w"]) for g in r2["regions"]]) / 1.5
        t2 = np.mean([np.linalg.norm(g["t"]) for g in r2["regions"]]) / 1.5
        w1 = np.mean([np.linalg.norm(g["w"]) for g in r1["regions"]]) / 1.5
        rows.append(dict(part=key, n=r2["n"], rot_p1=w1, rot_p2=w2, ratio=w2 / max(w1, 1e-12), t=t2, L=L, hand=w2 * L + t2,
                         vmax_p2=r2["vmax"], vm99_p2=r2["vm99"], vm99_p1=r1["vm99"]))
        print(rows[-1], flush=True)
    return rows


def bearing_terms():
    from .params import BASELINE as d
    from .parts import roll, shoulder
    a = d.arm
    reach = (a.l_upper + a.l_fore + a.l_hand) * 1000
    spans = {"pitch (61808 pair)": abs(shoulder.W_OUT - shoulder.W_IN), "roll (61808 pair)": abs((roll.W2[0] + roll.W2[1]) - (roll.W1[0] + roll.W1[1])) / 2,
             "yaw (61808 pair)": abs((roll.P1[0] + roll.P1[1]) - (roll.P2[0] + roll.P2[1])) / 2, "elbow (61808 + 6708)": 108.0}
    lev = {"pitch (61808 pair)": reach, "roll (61808 pair)": reach, "yaw (61808 pair)": reach, "elbow (61808 + 6708)": (a.l_fore + a.l_hand) * 1000}
    return [(k, s, 2 * PLAY_MM * SHIM / s * lev[k]) for k, s in spans.items()]


def main():
    from . import bones
    from .params import BASELINE
    rows = run()
    br = bearing_terms()
    tube = bones.score(BASELINE)[1]
    tot = sum(r["hand"] for r in rows) + sum(b[2] for b in br) + tube
    after = tot * (1 - COMP)
    L = ["# Hand-deflection compliance chain (generated by `python -m rnd.stiffness`)", "", __doc__.split("\n\n", 1)[1].strip(), "",
         f"**Static hand deflection, 1 kg straight out: {tot:.1f} mm uncompensated (target ≤ 6), {after:.1f} mm after a gravity model (target ≤ 2).** "
         f"{'✅' if tot <= 6 and after <= 2 else '❌'}", "",
         "## 1. Printed parts (P2 vs P1 on the same mesh)", "",
         "| Part | Elements | Seat rotation P1 → P2 (mrad, static) | P2/P1 | Seat translation (mm) | Lever to hand (mm) | Hand (mm) | Von Mises P2 max / 99.5 % (P1 99.5 %) |",
         "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    L += [f"| {r['part']} | {r['n']:,} | {r['rot_p1'] * 1000:.2f} → {r['rot_p2'] * 1000:.2f} | {r['ratio']:.2f} | {r['t']:.2f} | {r['L']:.0f} | {r['hand']:.2f} | "
          f"{r['vmax_p2']:.1f} / {r['vm99_p2']:.1f} ({r['vm99_p1']:.1f}) MPa |" for r in rows]
    L += ["", "## 2. Bearing play (shimmed pairs, 30 % of 10 µm C0 clearance)", "", "| Joint | Span (mm) | Hand (mm) |", "|---|---|---|"]
    L += [f"| {k} | {s:.0f} | {h:.2f} |" for k, s, h in br]
    L += ["", f"## 3. Carbon tubes (rnd/bones.py): {tube:.1f} mm", "",
          "**Reading:** P2 elements roughly double the P1 bending rotations here, as the review said; P1 FEA alone under-states the deflection. "
          "The stress margins in fea.md (P1, 99.5th percentile) are re-checked by the P2 column: compare with the 12 MPa across-layer allowable."]
    (DOCS / "stiffness.md").write_text("\n".join(L))
    (DOCS / "stiffness.json").write_text(json.dumps({"total_mm": tot, "after_mm": after, "rows": rows, "bearings": br, "tube": tube}))
    print("\n".join(L[4:8]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

=== FILE: rnd/balancer.py ===
```python
"""Shoulder-pitch gravity balancer: zero-free-length spring via cable (Anglepoise / Herder / French & Widden principle).

Geometry: pulley A sits on the torso a metres *above* the pitch axis O. Anchor B is a pin on the pitch joint's
big pulley, b metres from O along the arm (below O when the arm hangs). A cable runs B → over A → down into the
torso to a caged spring (A9: through a 2:1 tackle into a die spring). The cage is designed so the spring is at its free length when the cable length
A–B would be zero, so the spring force is k·|AB| ("zero free length").
Then the torque about O is k·a·b·sin(pitch) exactly, for any pitch: the same law the workspace study assumed.
K is adjusted by sliding pulley A along a slot (a changes), never by pre-tension (which would break the law).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np

from .params import BASELINE, Design

A_NOM, A_RANGE = 0.050, (0.045, 0.065)   # m, pulley A height above O (slot). |a-b| ≥ 15 mm so pin B clears pulley A at pitch 180°
B = 0.030                                # m, anchor pin radius on the balancer lever (loop 7 CAD: 40 → 30 so B clears A when the arm points up)
R_PULLEY = 0.0055                        # m, pulley A radius: U604ZZ grooved bearing pulley (A9: was an ungrooved Ø12 printed pulley)
CABLE_BREAK_N = 2000.0                   # 2 mm Dyneema/UHMWPE braid (ESTIMATE; typical 150–250 daN)
SPRING_SAFE_STROKE = 0.105               # m, cage stroke available (kept for the score signature)
# A9 (loop 40, external review finding 10): the loop-7 spring could not exist. Zero free length makes the spring deflect by
# the whole A–B span (up to a + b = 80 mm) at k = 3 N/mm: it stores ≈ 11 J, and a Ø25 compression spring that does that
# without overstress is ≈ 210 mm long (25-coil, d 3.2 mm) — it never fitted the 135 mm cage. Fix: a 2:1 tackle (the cable
# runs B → A → a moving pulley on the spring plate → anchored), so the spring sees half the travel at four times the rate,
# and a stock ISO 10243 die spring (rectangular wire, high energy density) fits the cage.
TACKLE = 2
CAGE_L = 0.135
DIE = dict(name="ISO 10243 light-load (green) die spring, Ø25 hole / Ø12.5 rod, L0 115 mm (ESTIMATE rate ≈ 12 N/mm; pick the catalogue rate ≥ 4·k)",
           L0=0.115, max_frac=0.40)        # long-life deflection limit for light-load die springs (≈ 40 % of L0, ESTIMATE)


def spring_rate(K: float, a: float = A_NOM, b: float = B) -> float:
    return K / (a * b)


def torque(theta: float, k: float, a: float, b: float, rp: float = R_PULLEY) -> float:
    """Exact spring torque about O (N·m) with a finite pulley at A. theta = pitch from hanging (rad)."""
    Bp = np.array([b * math.sin(theta), -b * math.cos(theta)])          # anchor (x forward, z up)
    Ap = np.array([0.0, a])
    d = Ap - Bp
    L = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    # tangent point on pulley A: the cable leaves B toward the pulley's near side; length to tangency
    l_t = math.sqrt(max(L * L - rp * rp, 1e-12))
    ang = math.atan2(d[1], d[0]) + math.asin(rp / L) * (1 if Bp[0] >= 0 else -1)
    u = np.array([math.cos(ang), math.sin(ang)])
    ext = l_t + rp * (math.pi / 2)          # wrap ≈ quarter turn into the torso (constant part folded into the cage)
    F = k * (ext - rp * math.pi / 2)        # cage zeroed so F = k·(free span length)
    return float(Bp[0] * F * u[1] - Bp[1] * F * u[0]), F, l_t


def study(d: Design = BASELINE):
    K = d.arm.spring_k_nm
    k = spring_rate(K)
    th = np.radians(np.linspace(-200, 60, 261))
    ideal = K * np.sin(th)
    real = np.array([torque(t, k, A_NOM, B)[0] for t in th])
    F = np.array([torque(t, k, A_NOM, B)[1] for t in th])
    span = np.array([torque(t, k, A_NOM, B)[2] for t in th])
    err = np.max(np.abs(real - ideal))
    adj = (k * A_RANGE[0] * B, k * A_RANGE[1] * B)
    ks = TACKLE ** 2 * k                                   # spring rate behind the tackle
    defl = float(span.max()) / TACKLE                      # spring deflection from free length (zero-free-length datum)
    ok = defl <= DIE["max_frac"] * DIE["L0"] and DIE["L0"] + 0.012 <= CAGE_L
    return dict(K=K, k=k, err=err, Fmax=float(F.max()), stroke=float(span.max() - span.min()), span_max=float(span.max()),
                adj=adj, cable_sf=CABLE_BREAK_N / F.max(), ks=ks, defl=defl, Fs=TACKLE * float(F.max()), energy=0.5 * k * float(span.max()) ** 2, fits=ok)


def report(d: Design = BASELINE) -> str:
    s = study(d)
    return "\n".join([
        "# Shoulder balancer (auto-generated by `python -m rnd.run_all`)", "",
        "Zero-free-length cable-spring balancer. Pulley A sits 45–65 mm above the pitch axis in a slot; pin B sits on a lever on the pitch "
        "shaft 30 mm from the axis. A cable runs B → A → (2:1 tackle) → a caged die spring in the torso, giving "
        "τ = k·a·b·sin(pitch) at every angle. Precedent: the Anglepoise lamp, Herder's spring balancers, French & Widden cable balancers.", "",
        "| Quantity | Value |", "|---|---|",
        f"| Design K (params) | {s['K']:.1f} N·m |",
        f"| Spring rate k = K/(a·b) | **{s['k'] / 1000:.2f} N/mm** |",
        f"| Max spring force (arm hanging) | {s['Fmax']:.0f} N |",
        f"| Cable span A–B, max (= zero-free-length spring travel without a tackle) | {s['span_max'] * 1000:.0f} mm; energy {s['energy']:.1f} J |",
        f"| **A9: 2:1 tackle → spring rate / deflection / force** | **{s['ks'] / 1000:.0f} N/mm / {s['defl'] * 1000:.0f} mm / {s['Fs']:.0f} N** |",
        f"| Die spring L0 {DIE['L0'] * 1000:.0f} mm: deflection {s['defl'] / DIE['L0']:.0%} of L0 (limit {DIE['max_frac']:.0%}); cage {CAGE_L * 1000:.0f} mm | {'✅' if s['fits'] else '❌'} |",
        f"| Law error from the Ø{2 * R_PULLEY * 1000:.0f} mm pulley (max over −200…60°) | {s['err']:.2f} N·m ({s['err'] / s['K']:.0%} of K) |",
        f"| K adjustment by sliding A ({A_RANGE[0] * 1000:.0f}–{A_RANGE[1] * 1000:.0f} mm) | {s['adj'][0]:.1f}–{s['adj'][1]:.1f} N·m |",
        f"| Cable safety (2 mm UHMWPE, ESTIMATE {CABLE_BREAK_N:.0f} N) | {s['cable_sf']:.0f}× |", "",
        f"**Spring (A9, loop 40):** {DIE['name']}. The loop-7 compression spring (3 N/mm over 85 mm, ≈ 11 J) would have been ≈ 210 mm long and never fitted the "
        "135 mm cage (external review finding 10). The moving pulley halves the travel; die springs store the energy in about half the length. "
        "Cable: B → U604 pulley A → U604 moving pulley on the spring plate → anchor screw on the cage.",
        "**Detach hazard (A9):** with the arm off, the spring torque K·sin(pitch) is unopposed. Detach and attach only with the arm hanging (pitch 0, "
        "where the torque is zero), with the **park pin** in: a Ø6 pin through the bracket wall into a hole in the balancer disc at pitch 0. The pitch motor holds the "
        "shaft as well while powered.",
        "**Failure mode:** with the cable cut, the arm is simply unbalanced; the pitch motor still holds it (see the workspace margins without the spring in spring.md)."])


def score(d: Design = BASELINE):
    s = study(d)
    return s["err"] / s["K"], s["fits"]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(report())

```

=== FILE: sw/safety.py ===
```python
"""Safety supervisor (S1-safety, SW2). Sits between any policy/teleop and the motor drivers; independent of the policy.

Every control cycle it takes the wanted joint targets and returns what may be sent, or a stop:
1. **Watchdog:** if `tick()` isn't called for WATCHDOG_S (50 ms), the next call returns STOP and the e-stop relay's
   watchdog input stops toggling (electrical.md A5: the contactor opens in hardware as well).
2. **Joint limits:** targets clamped to [lower + margin, upper − margin] (sw/description/joints.json, from the twin).
3. **Velocity limit:** target steps limited to v_max·dt per joint (bench default 1.5 rad/s at the joint).
4. **Torque limit:** feed-forward torque clamped to the joint's rated torque (peak only with `allow_peak`).
5. **Workspace:** the hand (forward kinematics from the bench twin) must stay inside a box above the table; a target
   that leaves it is rejected and the last good target is held.
6. **Feedback sanity:** a measured position outside the limits by more than 5°, a motor error flag, or a temperature
   over TEMP_C (55 °C, the PETG/ASA can rule, thermal.md A6) → STOP.
7. **E-stop input:** latched; needs `reset()` with the button released.
Every decision is counted, so a session log shows how often the policy was overruled.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

WATCHDOG_S, TEMP_C = 0.050, 55.0
DESC = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "description" / "joints.json"


@dataclass
class Verdict:
    stop: bool
    q: np.ndarray | None
    t_ff: np.ndarray | None
    reasons: list = field(default_factory=list)


class Supervisor:
    def __init__(self, v_max=1.5, margin_deg=3.0, ws_lo=(0.10, -0.45, 0.76), ws_hi=(0.80, 0.45, 1.35), clock=time.monotonic, fk=None):
        J = json.loads(DESC.read_text())
        self.names = [j["name"] for j in J]
        m = math.radians(margin_deg)
        self.lo = np.array([j["lower"] for j in J]) + m
        self.hi = np.array([j["upper"] for j in J]) - m
        self.rated = np.array([j["rated_nm"] for j in J])
        self.peak = np.array([j["peak_nm"] for j in J])
        self.v_max, self.clock, self.fk = v_max, clock, fk
        self.ws_lo, self.ws_hi = np.array(ws_lo), np.array(ws_hi)
        self.last_tick = None
        self.last_q = None
        self.estop = False
        self.counts = {}

    def _count(self, k):
        self.counts[k] = self.counts.get(k, 0) + 1

    def tick(self):
        self.last_tick = self.clock()

    def press_estop(self):
        self.estop = True

    def reset(self, q_meas):
        self.estop = False
        self.last_q = np.clip(np.asarray(q_meas, float), self.lo, self.hi)
        self.tick()

    def check(self, q_want, t_ff=None, q_meas=None, err=None, temp=None, dt=1 / 500, allow_peak=False) -> Verdict:
        now = self.clock()
        why = []
        if self.estop:
            why.append("e-stop latched")
        if self.last_tick is None or now - self.last_tick > WATCHDOG_S:
            why.append("watchdog")
        if q_meas is not None and (np.any(q_meas < self.lo - math.radians(8)) or np.any(q_meas > self.hi + math.radians(8))):
            why.append("measured position outside limits")
        if err is not None and np.any(np.asarray(err) != 0):
            why.append("motor error flag")
        if temp is not None and np.nanmax(np.asarray(temp, float)) > TEMP_C:
            why.append("over temperature")
        if why:
            for w in why:
                self._count(w)
            return Verdict(True, None, None, why)
        q = np.clip(np.asarray(q_want, float), self.lo, self.hi)
        if np.any(q != q_want):
            self._count("joint limit clamp")
        if self.last_q is None:
            self.last_q = q if q_meas is None else np.asarray(q_meas, float)
        step = self.v_max * dt
        q2 = self.last_q + np.clip(q - self.last_q, -step, step)
        if np.any(np.abs(q2 - q) > 1e-12):
            self._count("velocity clamp")
        if self.fk is not None:
            p = self.fk(q2)
            if np.any(p < self.ws_lo) or np.any(p > self.ws_hi):
                self._count("workspace reject")
                q2 = self.last_q
        lim = self.peak if allow_peak else self.rated
        t = None if t_ff is None else np.clip(np.asarray(t_ff, float), -lim, lim)
        if t_ff is not None and np.any(t != t_ff):
            self._count("torque clamp")
        self.last_q = q2
        return Verdict(False, q2, t, [])

```
